- **1915.01** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/neighbor-carries` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  Hermann Kral的煤桶磕在楼梯转角，煤块滚了一地。Klara坐在平台上，膝盖渗出血珠。Hermann Kral把桶一放，夹起Klara进了自家门，用温水冲伤口，又拿一块干面包塞过来。Josef Bauer赶来时，Klara正坐在厨房凳上啃面包。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (膝盖擦破，已冲洗); food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1917.02** `p9-childhood-household.half-bed/gave-wall-side` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  靠墙那块床板被炉火烘得温乎，Klara把枕头挪到床尾，让Elisabeth Bauer睡进去。门缝底下灌风，Klara把外套压在脚上，半夜还是冻醒了两回。Elisabeth Bauer倒没再滚下床，天亮时Klara的脚踝木得弯不过来，坐在床沿搓了半天。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (睡床尾受冷风); relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Anna Bauer在绳子那头喊。Klara踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Anna Bauer数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Klara把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Luise Bauer哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Klara蹲下把Luise Bauer驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Luise Bauer听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Klara的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Josef Bauer回来接过孩子，Klara扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000553:child_

  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus_

  Klara在操场边堵住Hedwig，问他从哪听来的。他先说是别人说的，后来承认是自己编的，因为Klara在课堂上答对了他答错的问题。Anna Bauer让他们各写一份说明，两份都锁进抽屉。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; flag +confronted_rumor_source; relation rival with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the classmate invented the rumour out of envy”_

- **1928.01** `r13-fam.help-homework::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/tears-on-the-page` tags: task, family, school, homework, sibling, literacy, reform, medical
  Luise Bauer把铅笔芯写断了两次，第三遍还是算错，眼泪滴在本子上，把墨迹洇开。Klara把本子拿开，说再写一遍，孩子却把椅子往后一推。母亲在灶边喊别吵，Klara只好把豆子收进碗里，听见Luise Bauer在床上抽鼻子。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +homework_ended_in_tears; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000553:child; condition_

- **1928.02** `r1-cul.fan-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/quoted-without-permission` tags: culture, letters, fandom, literacy, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara的信寄出半年，没有回音。后来Luise Kral 2在一篇文章里引用了这封信，没有署名，只写一个读者说。Klara在报上读到自己的句子，先是高兴，随后发现文章里的意思和自己写的相反。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation 0.03; flag +letter_quoted_without_permission; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; knowledge “the quoted sentence means the opposite of what was written”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1928.10** `r1-fam.sibling-rivalry::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rivalry.lost` tags: family, siblings, rivalry, apprenticeship, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Otto Bauer 2收了Emil。Klara在门口站到天黑，回家把父亲的工具箱打开，照着记忆里的样子练。Josef Bauer进来，说别跟你兄弟争，争来的东西不长久；Klara没有抬头，刨花落在脚背上，像一层薄雪。
  _effects: mood -0.10000000000000003; flag +lost_apprenticeship_to_sibling; role; skill; relation rival with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1929.01** `r14-edu.tutoring-sibling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/own-marks-slipped` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, tutoring, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Klara每晚给Luise Bauer讲题，自己的作业只能留到深夜。期中考试，Klara的名次掉了十几位，Maria Weiss在家长会上点名。回家后，Klara看见Luise Bauer趴在桌上睡着了，算术本还摊在胳膊下面，就没有叫醒。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +neglected_own_studies_

- **1929.02** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/coins-into-box` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Elisabeth Bauer的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Klara把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Elisabeth Bauer看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐); relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1929.03** `r3-teen.dropout-return::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/held-back` tags: education, dropout, return, poverty, class, reform, medical, schooling
  校长在名册上把Klara的名字划到低一年级，说缺的课不会自己长回来。Elisabeth Bauer已经比她高半个头，课间在走廊里喊她的旧绰号。Klara把书包带子绕在手腕上，绕了三圈。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +held_back_after_dropout; relation rival with person-elisabeth-bauer; education primary_

  邻居不肯在收入表上签字，说“签了就得罪人”。Josef Winter按规定把申请退回，Klara的奖学金停了一学期。Josef Bauer在楼道里骂了一句，邻居家的门始终没有开。Klara去教务处问能不能补交，窗口说“等下一年”。
  _effects: cash -6 (奖学金复核表); flag +scholarship_suspended_for_missing_testimony; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1929.12** `p10-edu-thresholds.technische-hochschule-entrance/drawing-fail` tags: adolescence, education, school, exam, apprenticeship, work, transition, technical_school
  制图室里全是铅笔划纸的声音，尺子压住纸边，他的手心把纸角压出了汗渍。收卷时他把错的地方又看了一遍，才把纸递出去。回家路上他把图纸卷成筒，握在手里不松。
  _effects: cash -3 (技术学院考试报名费); mood -0.15000000000000002; skill; flag +kept_failed_drawing; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1930.01** `major-work-first-employment/turned-away` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头翻完工票，问Klara上一个东家是谁。Klara说厂子关了，工头又问有没有辞工证明；没有证明，名册就不能写。后面的人挤上来，Klara被推到台阶下面。中午的钟响了，Klara站在街对面，看新来的人把名字一个个写上去。
  _effects: cash -2 (carfare and waiting); reputation -0.03; flag +no_work_references; role; mood -0.07000000000000006; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Franz Berg的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1930.01** `r15-history.street-assembly::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/scattered` tags: politics, assembly, organization, history, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  警察在第三个人发言时赶到。人群散得很快，Klara跟着别人拐进巷子，鞋底踩过一堆碎玻璃。第二天没有人被抓，但街角多了一个穿便衣的人。
  _effects: mood -0.019999999999999796; state_add political-suspect state:world-vienna-1912:65_

- **1930.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.accepted` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  厂门口的砖墙被太阳晒得发烫。Alois Berg在旁听着，插了一句这后生看着手稳。工头把烟灰弹进阴沟，说试半天，午饭他管。Klara点头时，肚子先响了一声。
  _effects: flag +trial_work_pending; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

  Otto Bauer 2翻到担保人一栏，说必须有两位老会员签字。Maria Kral愿意签一个，另一个名字写了又划掉。Otto Bauer 2把表推回来，说“不是不认老乡，是怕有人借了钱就走”。Klara把一克朗会费收回口袋，听见里屋有人在分汤。
  _effects: flag +refused_mutual_aid_membership; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; knowledge “membership requires two sponsors”_

  Franz Berg在几天后找到Klara，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; household; role; interaction_

  _effects: birth male; health -0.07999999999999996 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:73_

  代表团没有带来任命书，只带来一套戏服：市庆游行需要一个“国王”，一天两场，管一顿饭。Klara穿上戏服时，围观的人鼓起掌来。掌声是真的，王冠是假的，晚饭也是真的。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:74; cash 2 (游行演出的酬劳); flag +hired_as_festival_king; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

  _effects: flag +relative_refused_loan; relation kin with person-anna-bauer_

  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Klara和Franz Berg在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Franz Berg把煤钱推过来，Klara接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: birth female; health -0.22 (difficult childbirth); condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:82_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Anna Berg。接生的人收六克朗，Franz Berg在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Klara起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中5.2克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:100_

- **1934.01** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  Klara和Alois Berg在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Emil Bauer 3没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室（其中2克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.06; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:004651; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

- **1934.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004651_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara把孩子的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。孩子今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Klara听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 45 -> 53; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 39 -> 36; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara把孩子的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。孩子今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Klara听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 53 -> 61; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 36 -> 33; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1934.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Klara走到Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1934.04** `r3-mig.remittance-failure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::war-severed-post/returned-address-unknown` tags: migration, remittance, family, failure, money, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hermann Bauer把汇款单和一张退条一起推过来，退条上盖着“地址不明”。Klara说上个月还寄到过，Hermann Bauer说“上个月是上个月”。五克朗扣了两克朗手续费，退回来的钱只够买三天的面包。
  _effects: cash -5 (退回来的汇款单（其中5克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018; condition; flag +remittance_returned_address_unknown; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; knowledge “returned remittances lose the fee twice”_

- **1934.10** `r13-rel.separation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::separation-war/mobilized-before-the-quarrel-ended` tags: relationship, separation, household, conflict, marriage, vienna, urban, crisis
  两人的争吵还没说完，Franz Berg的征召令就贴到了门上。Klara替Franz Berg收拾行李时，把那件没补完的衬衣也塞了进去。Franz Berg在门口站了一会儿，说等回来再吵，Klara没有笑。
  _effects: role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; move place-wien-printshop-winter_

- **1935.01** `r14-edu.sibling-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-passed` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, exam, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Luise Bauer从考场出来，手里攥着准考证，说最后一道题做出来了。放榜那天，Klara陪Luise Bauer去看榜，名字在中间偏下，够了。Luise Bauer跳起来，又立刻站好，回头问Klara能不能把旧课本留下。报名费两克朗，Klara替Luise Bauer交了。
  _effects: cash -2 (弟弟妹妹的准考证（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +sibling_passed_exam; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000553:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002985_

- **1935.01** `r15-wish.faction-price::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/grew` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, intrigue, office, vienna, urban
  俱乐部从十一人变成四十人。Klara仍然坐主席的位置，但每次开会前都要先和Rosa对一遍名单。有人开始叫Klara“主席”，也有人只在需要签字时才来。
  _effects: reputation 0.07999999999999996; knowledge “俱乐部扩大后，真正的权力不在主席的椅子上，而在会前对名单的修改。”; flag +faction_grew_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004651_

- **1935.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Hermann Berg。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Franz Berg把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Klara躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:104_

- **1935.01** `absurd15-wish.crown-for-a-day::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/festival-king-only` tags: wish, power, crown, office, irony, festival, vienna, urban
  代表团没有带来任命书，只带来一套戏服：市庆游行需要一个“国王”，一天两场，管一顿饭。Klara穿上戏服时，围观的人鼓起掌来。掌声是真的，王冠是假的，晚饭也是真的。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:105; cash 2 (游行演出的酬劳); flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1935.01** `r1-work.porter-luggage::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/found-the-missing-case` tags: work, porter, station, luggage, accusation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anna Huber说箱子是棕色的，Klara在行李房里一排排看号牌，最后在最底层找到一只被压歪的箱子。锁扣开了，里面的银餐具还在。Anna Huber把两克朗塞进Klara手里，Gustav Berg在门口记下时间，笔尖在纸上停了一停。
  _effects: cash 2 (行李房里的十七号牌); flag +found_missing_luggage; role; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000343:child_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara被叫到学校，老师说孩子这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Klara没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 64 -> 59; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 33 -> 38; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara被叫到学校，老师说孩子这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Klara没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 59 -> 54; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 38 -> 43; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1935.01** `r13-work.unemployment-benefit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/benefit-approved` tags: work, unemployment, benefit, institution, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil Bauer 3把Klara的工龄册翻到最后一页，又对照了厂里寄来的解雇单，说可以领十二周。钱不多，每周够买面包和煤。Klara在表格上按了手印，Emil Bauer 3把一张薄薄的领取券推过来，说下周同一时间来，别迟到。
  _effects: cash 6 (补助金窗口的第二张表); flag +work13_unemployment_benefit_approved; knowledge “the benefit runs for twelve weeks and must be collected in p”_

- **1935.01** `initiative:found_process:org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:004651`
  权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。
  _effects: historical process 权力俱乐部的持续运动; historical development:initiative-org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:004651-1935 as organizer; historical development:initiative-org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:004651-1935 as organizer; knowledge “权力俱乐部的持续运动不是一次会议，而是我们这些年积累出来的持续组织。”; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1935.01** `historical_phase`
  历史进程《权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。
  _effects: knowledge “我亲历或知晓「权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招”; knowledge “我亲历或知晓「权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005226_

- **1935.03** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-humiliation` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +wish:power:wounded_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004893_

- **1935.08** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说孩子这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 61 -> 64; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005101_

- **1935.11** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sibling-medicine-bill/refuse-push-bill` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara把药费单从糖罐底下抽出来，对折，推回桌子中央，说上个月的工钱已经贴过煤钱，这回一个赫勒也拿不出。Anna Bauer的脸沉下来，练习本翻得哗哗响，最后把单子重新压回糖罐底下，起身去够门后的外套。三只咖啡杯里的渣子凉在桌上，兄妹这一回把话都说薄了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Franz Berg在楼梯间坐了很久；Klara在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Klara的名字；此后邻居提起Klara，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1936.01** `curse15-bargain.borrowed-name::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/a-key-and-a-title` tags: wish, power, curse, ritual, bargain, office, irony, vienna
  办事处后门的Emil Bauer 3把一份旧名册翻到空页，让Klara在“会长”后面签下名字。三天后，Klara真的收到一枚铜钥匙和一张聘书：夜校同学会会长，职责是保管储物间。同学会只有三个成员，储物间的锁已经坏了。Emil Bauer 3没有再出现。
  _effects: organization_found 夜校同学会 (other); position 夜校同学会会长 @org:curse15-borrowed:evt:world-vienna-1912:005423; state_add curse-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:116; reputation 0.030000000000000027; cash -1 (聘书与印花的费用（其中1克朗记入债务）)_

- **1936.01** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Klara把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Gustav Berg站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Klara手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; reputation 0.030000000000000027_

- **1937.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Klara手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.06000000000000001 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

- **1937.07** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-scandal-helps` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  照片登出后，Klara收到十七封邀请函，都是请去讲“如何面对舆论”。一个工商业协会送来一笔“研究资助”，请Klara去讲一次闭门课；课酬刚好补上办公室这个月的缺口。
  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044; organization_funds; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:124; flag +scandal_turned_into_speaking_fee; knowledge “十七封邀请函里，有十二封问的是同一件事：能不能把丑闻讲成经验。”; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1937.09** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sinecure-created` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Klara在编制表最下面添了一行“顾问”，月薪两克朗，职责是“必要时提供咨询”。Luise Kral第二天就搬来一张靠窗的桌子，带来一盆天竺葵；一个月后，Gustav Berg发现顾问唯一经手的文件是自己的午餐菜单。
  _effects: position 顾问 @org:curse15-borrowed:evt:world-vienna-1912:005423; organization_funds; relation friend with person-luise-kral; flag +private_sinecure; knowledge “顾问岗位没有固定职责，考勤由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说孩子这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/flees` tags: politics, persecution, 1938, migration
  Klara在夜里收拾了一只箱子，把证件缝进外套内衬，搭上往西的火车。Gustav Berg第二天来查人，只看到空床和没关严的窗。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -20 (escape and lodging（其中20克朗记入债务）); condition_

  圣文生会的修女在登记簿上写下Klara的门牌号，递过面包券和一小袋土豆。Friedrich Winter 2在旁边按了指印作保，说这户男人在工厂有活。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

  炉门一开，黑烟先扑出来，Klara眯着眼把纸团往里塞。火苗舔着碎煤慢慢亮起来，Anton Huber 3隔着门喊当心一氧化碳。水壶开始咕噜作响，屋里才有点热乎气。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

  Klara发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Friedrich Winter 2问是谁动的。Friedrich Winter 2说是Anton Huber 3嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1938.02** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  洗衣妇把工服从报纸里抖出来，翻开领口看了看油垢，点头收下。Klara转身回屋，铜板在围裙口袋里磕出轻响。Mitzi Berg还在楼梯上嘟囔，说这钱不如买半斤猪油。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000973:child_

  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

- **1941.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.relative-introduction/relative-introduction.never-mentions` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, relative, introduction
  点心纸包搁在膝上，Luise Weiss 2说熟人多，慢慢问，别急。Klara回家把这些话学给母亲听，母亲只叹了口气。那包糖后来自己家里分着吃了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; cash -1 (再买一包糖上门（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1941.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Luise Berg在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Luise Berg敲开Klara的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1941.06** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Alois Berg排在前面，回头替Klara看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Klara用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1941.08** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把通知念给Rosa听，Rosa摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Klara去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1941.10** `p9-history-medical-daily.sanitation-inspection/sign-and-demand-repair` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  签字时Klara把笔握得很紧，在“住户意见”一栏写了“下水道堵塞，垃圾箱无盖”。Ferdinand Stein念了一遍，说会转给房东；Josef Weiss 2凑过来看，问要不要按手印。
  _effects: reputation 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; knowledge “卫生巡查员把下水道堵塞记进了记录，说会通知房东限期修”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

  Klara刚提一句“那年分东西”，Anna Bauer就把勺子放下，说柜子是他先看中的，凭什么让。话越说越硬，汤还剩半碗，Anna Bauer起身拿了帽子，椅子在石地上刮出一声。Klara一个人坐到教堂的钟敲完，把两份饭钱都付了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-anna-bauer; cash -0.4 (付了两份饭钱); mood -0.06000000000000005_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 100 -> 100; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Klara走到半路风忽然大起来，围巾抽在脸上生疼，她掉头往回走。路过面包店时橱窗里还剩几个昨天的圆面包，四赫勒一个，她买了两个，揣在怀里带回去当晚饭。
  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个)_

- **1942.09** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Alois Berg 2没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Klara听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1942.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Klara摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Anton Winter第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Klara只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1942.12** `vienna-adult-social.sunday-table/sunday-table.argument` tags: adulthood, social, work, family, partnership
  星期天中午，Klara和Franz Berg在出租屋的厨房桌边分面包，话题从房租转到Klara母亲上个月的药钱。Franz Berg把刀放下，说这个家不能总先填那边的窟窿。面包凉了，汤也没再热。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  Klara被叫到学校，老师说孩子这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Klara没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 100 -> 95; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 32 -> 37; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: condition; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1943.06** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-picked-coal` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  多瑙河码头的雾还没散，工头捏着名单念了Klara的名字。煤灰落在汗湿的脖颈上，Anna Berg 2在铁门边看着，说这活儿一天顶三天，可肩膀得疼上一个礼拜。
  _effects: cash 4.2 (卸煤半日的工钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

- **1943.10** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-deposit` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  窗子推开只有一线天光，墙纸边角翘起，Klara用指甲一刮便掉下一层湿粉。看门人揣好定金，说这间是朝北的，冬天火炉要添得勤些。Josef Weiss 2站在楼梯口没进屋，只替Klara看着箱子。
  _effects: cash -4 (当场付下定金和钥匙钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); residence_

- **1943.11** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction

- **1943.12** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.notary-inheritance/sign-away-for-cash` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara在Ferdinand Stein面前签下放弃继承的声明，Josef Bauer从内袋里摸出二十克朗推过来，纸币上还带着体温。Anna Bauer把房契往自己那边挪了挪。Klara数也没数就把钱收好，出门时听见公证人办公室的门在背后合上，铜把手凉了一下手心，这二十克朗把兄妹之间的话买断了一截。
  _effects: cash 20 (放弃继承换来一笔现钱); relation kin with person-anna-bauer; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +renounced_inheritance_

- **1944.01** `r14-edu.sibling-favour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sibling-fell-ill` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, reunion, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Luise Bauer病倒的消息是邻居带来的。Klara赶到时，屋里只有一张床和一只药罐，孩子坐在门槛上。Klara留下来照顾了半个月，把自己的夜校报名费垫进了药钱。Luise Bauer退烧那天，叫了Klara一声小名。药钱六克朗，是Klara垫的。
  _effects: cash -6 (多年后的一封家信（其中6克朗记入债务）); flag +cared_for_sick_sibling; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000553:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004862_

  会议室里有人念了一份动议，说Klara把预算当成人情。Ferdinand Stein把签名页传了一圈，最后停在自己手里：支持票比反对票少三张。Klara没有发言，只把下一季度的支出表放在桌上，表上每一笔都有收据。
  _effects: reputation 0.040000000000000036; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; flag +survived_removal_motion; knowledge “动议只拿到少数支持，三名原本签名的委员在投票前撤回了。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1944.01** `initiative:found_process:org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:008993`

  _effects: historical process 接手后的俱乐部的持续运动; historical development:initiative-org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:008993-1944 as organizer; historical development:initiative-org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:008993-1944 as organizer; knowledge “接手后的俱乐部的持续运动不是一次会议，而是我们这些年积累出来的持续组织。”; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1944.10** `r3-crime.victim-family::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::military-court-jurisdiction/charges-filed` tags: crime, family, grief, court, settlement, vienna, urban, crisis
  Ferdinand Stein来灵堂的时候，香还没烧完。Anna Bauer把状纸递过去，上面写到一半，墨迹被眼泪晕开了。Ferdinand Stein看完，说“可以立案”，但需要两个证人。Hermann Weiss在门口说“我看见了”，Anna Bauer忽然跪下去，被Ferdinand Stein一把扶住。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; condition; flag +pressed_charges_for_family; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; knowledge “the prosecutor needs two witnesses to file the case”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

- **1944.11** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1944.12** `office16-hire.protege::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hired-competent` tags: office, hire, patronage, bureaucracy, work, power, vienna, urban
  Klara把Elisabeth Bauer的名字写在编制表的空行上，又在旁边注了“试用三个月”。Ferdinand Stein提醒说预算只够半个位置，Klara还是把名字留下了。第二天，Elisabeth Bauer提前半小时到，把走廊里的旧档案按年份重新排了一遍。

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/displaced` tags: war, liberation, 1945, migration
  炮火把Klara住的那条街烧掉一半。Klara跟着人群走到城外，在临时收容所登记，床位上铺的是发霉的稻草。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -5 (temporary shelter（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

  Klara把孩子的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。孩子今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Klara听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 100 -> 100; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 33 -> 30; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Klara数了数前面攒动的人头，把口袋里的硬币又攥紧了些，转身离开队伍。走到街角的教堂时，管风琴正从敞开的门里传出来，Klara在最后一排长凳上坐下，听完整段前奏曲，一个赫勒也没花，只跪得膝盖发酸。出门时与Luise Huber道了别，约好下回还来排队。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002199:child_

- **1945.02** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household
  Klara把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Klara的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart（其中8克朗记入债务）)_

  _effects: condition; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000553:child_

  Klara用三个月把账本上的零碎支出并成四类，又停掉两份没有人看的简报。年底结账时，Katharina Weiss发现不仅没有赤字，还剩下够换一批档案柜的钱；没有剪彩，只有仓库里新柜子的木头味。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.010000000000000009; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +balanced_the_books; knowledge “停掉两份简报、合并零碎支出后，年底还剩一笔够换档案柜的钱。”_

- **1950.08** `r2-law.inheritance-tax-audit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/tax-assessed` tags: law, inheritance, tax, audit, farm, vienna, urban, postwar
  Katharina Weiss把遗产清单念到“耕犁一把、母牛一头”，问Klara为什么没有估地价。Klara说地是租的，Katharina Weiss说租约也是财产。Emil在旁边补了一句：租约还有七年。Katharina Weiss把七年租金折成现价，写进税基，Klara看着数字，手里的清单卷了边。
  _effects: cash -26 (遗产清单上的犁（其中26克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +inheritance_tax_included_lease_value; relation rival with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “a lease with seven years left counts as taxable property”_

- **1950.10** `r2-rural.well-contamination::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::reform-new-pump/signed-for-the-pump` tags: rural, water, health, neighbor, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien的人带来一台手压泵，说公署出三分之二，剩下的按户摊。Klara把分摊的钱数念给Anton Winter听，Anton Winter说这比淘井便宜。装泵那天，孩子们围着铁管，等第一股水出来。
  _effects: cash -5 (井里的死老鼠（其中3克朗记入债务）); flag +installed_village_pump; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; organization_membership_

- **1951.01** `curse15-bargain.borrowed-name::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-price-is-a-name` tags: wish, power, curse, ritual, bargain, office, irony, vienna
  Grete Bauer只要Klara在一张空白委任状上写下真名，说剩下的由办事处去办。一个月过去，职位没有来，Klara的名字却出现在一份领款单上，金额和签名都不属于Klara。Grete Bauer调走了，窗口换了人，没有人愿意查那张纸是从哪一叠里抽出来的。
  _effects: cash -2 (打点办事处的钱（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +name_used_without_office; reputation -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001321:child; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1951.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012530_

- **1951.03** `r2-law.wage-garnishment::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/exempt-minimum` tags: law, garnishment, wages, debt, court, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara拿着工资单去找Grete Bauer，说扣完三成就低于法定最低生活费。Grete Bauer算了一遍，把扣押比例改成两成，又让接手后的俱乐部出具家庭人口证明。接手后的俱乐部不愿意写，Grete Bauer说那就按两成先扣；Klara把改过的单子折好，发现少扣的一成刚好够孩子的鞋。
  _effects: cash 2 (工资扣押令); flag +garnishment_reduced_to_minimum; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001321:child; knowledge “garnishment cannot push wages below the statutory minimum”_

- **1951.06** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把通知念给Luise Kral听，Luise Kral摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Klara去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.12** `kernel-health.street-accident::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/accident-fatal` tags: health, accident, tram, insurance, street, vienna, urban, postwar
  马车夫刹不住车，Klara被挤在车轮和路沿之间。Hermann Bauer跑去叫警察，回来时人已经不动了；事故报告上写着“行人横穿”，赔偿金刚好够一副棺材。棺材铺的人来量尺寸时，街上的雪还没扫。车夫在一边搓着手，没说话。
  _effects: death (crushed chest in a street collision); cash 12 (small compensation assessed as pedestrian fault); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1914.01** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/wet-floor` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Alois Lechner端盆出去倒水，灶边淌了一地。Mitzi在摇床里翻身，摇床被湿地板滑得挪了位，撞在桌脚上。Alois Lechner回来时孩子哭得满脸通红，摇床腿松了一条。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (摇床撞上桌脚，受了惊); cash -0.3 (请楼下木匠紧摇床腿); mood -0.03_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Mitzi拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Franz Lechner弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Mitzi接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Mitzi前面的大婶一直往后靠，Mitzi就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Mitzi的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.02 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1919.05** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/vinegar-compress` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  碗里倒上醋，兑一半凉水，Alois Lechner把布巾浸透，拧到不滴水，敷在Mitzi的额头上。布一热就换，换下来的搭在床沿。Mitzi半夜说梦话，Alois Lechner用手背试了试温度，又去换一盆水。天亮时枕布湿了一片，Alois Lechner的手指白得像泡过石灰。
  _effects: health 0.03 (湿敷后退了烧); mood 0.02; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Grete Stein从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Mitzi。Mitzi把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1920.07** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Mitzi在洗衣房的长凳上睡着，醒来时喉咙像塞了棉花。Alois Lechner把最后的干净布巾浸了凉水，敷在Mitzi额头上，整夜换了七八回。第四天Mitzi能坐起来喝粥，下嘴唇脱下一小片皮，Alois Lechner用指尖替Mitzi拈掉。
  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Mitzi后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Mitzi没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Mitzi用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.029999999999999995; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Ferdinand Winter在绳子那头喊。Mitzi踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Ferdinand Winter数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Mitzi把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter_

- **1922.11** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Mitzi被推到拱廊柱子边，围裙带子绕了两圈，系在铁环上。Mitzi一挣，肩膀撞上石柱，整条胳膊麻了一下。Anna Weiss提着菜篮过来，把篮子往地上一放，从怀里摸出削土豆的小刀，挑开绳结，没跟那两个孩子说一句话。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000257:child; mood -0.05; condition_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Alois Lechner把Mitzi拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Mitzi把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Mitzi蹲下把两包并排，按Alois Lechner说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; skill_

- **1923.07** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书在Mitzi的书包里藏了三天，课间才敢翻两页。还书那天，Mitzi把书角淋皱的事先说了，又把书页一张张抚平。Grete Stein翻到最后一页，看了看书脊，说下次别带进院子，书却还是借给了Mitzi。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +wish:knowledge:pursuing_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/parent-covers-with-coat` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Lechner半夜起来添煤，看见Mitzi趴在桌上睡着，把外套盖在Mitzi肩上，又把错题本翻到折角那页。第二天Mitzi提前半小时到校，借Maria Weiss的粉笔在黑板上重做了一遍。考试结果出来，Mitzi比录取线多出四分，Alois Lechner把外套上的煤灰拍干净，才陪Mitzi去报到。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1925.06** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1925.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1925.11** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.ask-a-mentor/borrowed-specimen-book` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Emil Bauer 2说自己眼睛不行了，带不了徒弟，但从帆布袋里抽出一本旧字样本，让Mitzi拿回去临摹。书皮用麻线缝过三次，扉页上有一行褪色的签名。少年双手接过来，在咖啡馆门口鞠了个躬。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; flag +typesetting_specimen_book; cash -1 (咖啡馆里请老师傅喝一杯); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1926.03** `r2-faith.parish-school-fee::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/free-place-granted` tags: faith, school, charity, education, fees, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Luise Kral 2在学费簿上找到Mitzi的名字，用红笔在旁边写了“免”。Franz Stein说课本还得自己买，Luise Kral 2就从柜子里翻出一本旧算术，封面写着上一个学生的名字。Mitzi把书抱回家，Alois Lechner用报纸包了书皮，说念完这本再说。
  _effects: flag +received_free_parish_school_place; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; knowledge “the parish keeps a red-ink list of fee exemptions”; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

  Mitzi的信寄出半年，没有回音。后来Friedrich Winter 2在一篇文章里引用了这封信，没有署名，只写一个读者说。Mitzi在报上读到自己的句子，先是高兴，随后发现文章里的意思和自己写的相反。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999999; reputation 0.03; flag +letter_quoted_without_permission; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; knowledge “the quoted sentence means the opposite of what was written”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1926.11** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Mitzi推刨子。Mitzi推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Mitzi，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Alois Lechner在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

  Mitzi跟着Marie Huber走进印刷厂后面的屋子，交了十赫勒会费，在借书簿上按了手印。他挑了一本讲蒸汽机的册子，封面烫金已经磨掉；管理员说下月十赫勒要按时交，书才能续借。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-marie-huber; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.04000000000000001_

  _effects: health 0.05 (种痘室门口的袖子); condition; flag +vaccinated_in_school_campaign; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

  _effects: cash -3 (派出所里的第一次签字); mood -0.08; flag +fined_by_police; relation rival with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; skill; mood 0.020000000000000004_

  Karl Kraus把杯子推回来，说摔坏的东西粘得再好也有缝。Mitzi在门口站了一会儿，把杯子放进外套口袋，杯沿磕在扣子上，响了一声。回家的路上，Mitzi绕开了两人常走的那条街。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +friendship_unrepaired_

- **1930.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Elisabeth Bauer嫌Mitzi的床单滴水滴到Elisabeth Bauer家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Mitzi下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Mitzi把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Elisabeth Bauer那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-elisabeth-bauer; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

- **1930.04** `kernel-social.tenement-rumor::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/tenement-rumor.station-story` tags: social, rumor, reputation, neighbor, housing, everyday, reform, medical
  门房说Mitzi家的女儿在车站跟一个穿军装的人上了车。话传到三楼时，女儿还没回家；Mitzi去问Karl Kraus，Karl Kraus说只是看见她替人提箱子。Friedrich Winter 2在厨房门口听着，把面包掰成两半，谁也没接。
  _effects: reputation -0.05; role; flag +rumor_met; knowledge “话是从洗衣房传出来的，门房只是把它说给了三楼。”_

- **1930.08** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Josef Bauer没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Mitzi听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1931.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Mitzi难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Mitzi Huber；Mitzi一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Emil Huber把钟表送进当铺。Mitzi落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:75_

  Mitzi在石板上写自己的名字，写到第三遍才把最后一笔收住。Maria Weiss把Mitzi的手按在粉笔上，说慢慢来。放学时，Mitzi把石板抱在怀里，经过肉铺时第一次看懂招牌上写的是“今日有肝”。那天晚上，Mitzi在账本封皮上又写了一遍，写到最后一笔时手没有抖。
  _effects: mood 0.08000000000000002; flag +learned_to_sign_name; skill_

- **1932.07** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/keys-and-photo-returned` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Mitzi把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Lena把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.09000000000000002; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly_

  招工纸上的工钱写得明白，一天三克朗八十赫勒。Mitzi怕再等下去名额没了，从人缝里挤到前面，后面的Emil Winter一把拽住袖子。门房抬头看了看乱糟糟的队伍，把玻璃窗拉上了。

  Mitzi的接榫敲裂了。Maria Kral 2把废料扔进柴堆，说再练一年。Mitzi没有争辩，晚上留下来把裂口重新刨平。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +failed_journeyman_test; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 1 -> 2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

  收容所把一批难民送到乡下，Luise Weiss 2被分到葡萄园做工。她去看时，对方正在搬筐，手上全是划痕；农场主说“管饭就不错了”，她把自己带的奶酪留下。她离开时，Luise Weiss 2把一只编好的草篮塞给她。

  Mitzi在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg生下一个男婴，取名Alois Bauer。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Anton把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Mitzi躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:89_

  Maria Stein来收保费时，Mitzi正在修煤油炉。Maria Stein把保单翻到背面，指着一条小字说，明火取暖不在承保范围。Mitzi问那冬天怎么办，Maria Stein说可以加保，也可以把炉子搬到院子里；Franz Hofer在隔壁听见，笑了一声。
  _effects: cash -3 (保单背面的小字); flag +fire_policy_excludes_kerosene_stove; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; knowledge “the fire policy excludes open-flame heating”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

  他先租了合住的床位，一间屋三张床，和两个工人分摊炉煤。租金最便宜，押金只要两克朗，可夜里有人打呼、有人半夜回来踢到痰盂。他把工具袋压在枕头下，睡了第一夜。
  _effects: cash -3 (合住床位的押金与首周租金（其中3克朗记入债务）); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; knowledge “利奥波德城的合住床位一周约一克朗，押金两克朗”_

- **1934.05** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Anton Huber 3在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

  Mitzi没有再修钟，把上工的时间交给楼下教堂的钟声。风从多瑙河那边刮过来时，钟声就散，窗户一关更听不见。有一回她误了钟点，到车间时工头已经在门口站着，扣了半个克朗。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (迟到被扣工钱); mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1935.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Mitzi在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Mitzi把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004638_

- **1935.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, injury
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Anton在楼梯间坐了很久；Mitzi在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Mitzi的名字；此后邻居提起Mitzi，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: health -0.05 (stillbirth); cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）); role_

- **1935.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/settle-the-account` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  七点刚过，Alois Berg 2照例来买一只 Semmel。Mitzi把紫罗兰和两只 Semmel 放在柜台上，先付了四十赫勒，又把上回算错的账一笔一笔说清：借出去的是八克朗，记成了十克朗，是自己记错了。Alois Berg 2听完没马上说话，把零钱数进围裙口袋，最后说中午过来喝碗汤。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一小束紫罗兰和两只 Semmel（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; mood 0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1935.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi没有立刻答话，先去敲Grete Kral 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Grete Kral 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Mitzi把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1935.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Mitzi靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费（其中0.5克朗记入债务）)_

- **1935.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Mitzi在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Rosa从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.03 (左手背烫伤)_

- **1936.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa嫌Mitzi的床单滴水滴到Rosa家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Mitzi下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Mitzi把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Rosa那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005054_

- **1936.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle
  Mitzi和Anton在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Anton把煤钱推过来，Mitzi接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: mood -0.02999999999999997_

  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

  收容所门前排的队比平时多了一倍，Luise Kral把两张床并成一张，让两个女人各睡一头。Mitzi半夜醒来，发现旁边的人正把毯子往自己这边拉；两人谁也没有说话，最后各盖一半。

  Mitzi在坡下看见一副被丢弃的滑雪板，板上刻着部队番号。Katharina Weiss说这副板他认识，主人上个月没回来。Anna Bauer把板子收进屋里，说等春天再还给家属。Mitzi把自己的蜡借给那副板，擦了一遍，像在擦一件遗物。

  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; flag +funeral_society_member_

  _effects: mood -0.14; relation rival with person-franz-lechner; knowledge “遗产诉讼费要三十克朗，办事处不受理私人纠纷”_

- **1939.01** `r13-fam.help-neighbour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/carried-the-coal` tags: task, social, neighbor, help, care, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi看见Gustav Berg在楼梯口歇了三回，便把煤筐接过来，一筐一筐抬上三楼。最后一趟，Mitzi的腰在门框上撞了一下，Gustav Berg从柜里摸出一小瓶李子酒，硬倒了一盅。
  _effects: food; flag +carried_neighbour_coal; role; relation friend with person-gustav-berg_

- **1939.05** `r3-war.occupation-scrip::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/traded-eggs-for-scrip` tags: war, occupation, currency, black_market, fraternization, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton用占领军发的纸马克买Mitzi的鸡蛋，纸币上印着一只鹰，摸起来像旧报纸。Mitzi按黑市价算，Anton又加了一包烟；Otto Winter 2在门口看着，说“别让Franz Bauer看见”。交易完，Mitzi把纸马克压在米缸底下。
  _effects: cash 4 (占领军口袋里的纸马克); food; flag +traded_eggs_for_occupation_scrip; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; knowledge “occupation scrip buys eggs at black-market rates”_

- **1939.11** `r3-war.victory-empty-chair::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/celebrated-victory-with-grief` tags: war, victory, celebration, grief, community, vienna, urban, crisis
  停战消息传来时，广场上的人把帽子扔上天。Mitzi跟着喊了两声，忽然看见Anton Huber 3站在人群外，手里拿着一张折起来的阵亡通知。Otto Bauer 2把酒瓶塞给Mitzi，说“喝”，Mitzi喝了一口，酒是苦的。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; flag +celebrated_victory_with_grief; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; knowledge “the neighbor received a death notice that morning”_

- **1940.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Gustav Bauer。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Anton把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Mitzi躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:160_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `kernel-migrate.emigration-papers::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/civic-emigration-refused` tags: migration, papers, bureaucracy, port, emigration, vienna, urban, crisis
  她的申请被退回，理由是“担保人收入不足”。Emil Bauer 3说可以找第二担保人；她翻遍通讯录，发现能签字的人不是已经走了，就是不肯再担保。回家后，她把通讯录上能签字的名字一个个划掉。
  _effects: flag +emigration_papers_refused; cash -2 (照片、表格和往返车钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; reputation -0.010000000000000002; knowledge “护照申请需要担保人收入证明，找不到第二担保人就办不下去”_

- **1940.06** `r3-crime.domestic-theft::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/pawn-ticket-found` tags: crime, family, poverty, shame, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton在米缸后面摸到一张当票，日期是上周三，正是Mitzi说去排队买煤的那天。银勺是婚礼上Alois Lechner送的，勺柄上刻着两个人的名字。Anton把当票放在桌上，问“这是第几张”，没有等回答，只把自己的被子抱到了Franz Lechner家。
  _effects: cash 2 (米缸后面藏着一只银勺); mood -0.20000000000000007; condition; flag +stole_from_own_household; relation_end partner with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the silver spoon was pawned on a coal-queue day”_

- **1940.08** `state_reaction:wish-knowledge/knowledge-humiliation` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_reaction

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007075_

- **1940.10** `r3-war.armistice-eleven::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/fired-last-round` tags: war, armistice, ceasefire, aftermath, memory, vienna, urban, crisis
  停火前十分钟，Emil Bauer 3还让打最后一轮。Mitzi把炮弹推进膛，手却停在拉火绳上；Luise Berg 2说“别打了”，Mitzi说“不打要上军事法庭”。炮响后，对面没有还击，只有一只钢盔从壕边滚下去。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +fired_last_round_before_armistice; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; knowledge “the last shell was fired minutes before the armistice”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

  Emil Bauer 3按国籍把人分成三队，Mitzi被推到“无国籍”那一队。登记卡上只写了姓、年龄和一双鞋的尺码。第三排的草垫已经被人占了，Katharina Weiss挪出半个位置，说“夜里冷，别脱鞋”。
  _effects: condition; flag +registered_in_transit_camp; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; knowledge “the camp sorts people by nationality, not by family”_

  _effects: flag +looking_for_cheaper_housing; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Mitzi把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; skill_

- **1941.09** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/second-bridge-promise` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  在第二个桥洞下，Berta Berg 3先停下来，用鞋尖碾一颗烤栗子壳，说下星期天还是这个时候、还是这座桥。Mitzi点头，掏出四赫勒买了两小袋栗子，两人分着剥，手上都是糖渍；桥洞上的水还在滴，谁也没有提万一明天下雨怎么办。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子（其中0.04克朗记入债务）); mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱（其中0.3克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-elisabeth-bauer; reputation 0.04_

- **1942.01** `hist15-occ.work-permit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/permit-granted` tags: occupation, work, permit, institution, shortage, history, vienna, urban
  Ferdinand Stein在通行证上盖了章，又用铅笔在角落写了一个日期。Mitzi拿到许可，可以去城西的修配厂上工；走出劳动局时，Mitzi把证件摸了三遍，确认它还在口袋里。
  _effects: cash -2 (通行证手续费（其中2克朗记入债务）); role; flag +work_permit_granted; knowledge “城西修配厂只认盖蓝章的通行证。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1942.01** `r13-fam.arrange-childcare::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::childcare-war/factory-creche` tags: task, care, childcare, work, neighbor, wages, vienna, urban
  军工厂新设了托儿所，只收上夜班的女工的孩子。Mitzi抱着Gustav Bauer排了一早晨，登记的人问孩子有没有出过麻疹，又让Mitzi把工牌摘下来看。名额批下来时，Mitzi发现自己排在名单最后一个，前面还有二十多个名字被划掉。
  _effects: cash -2 (上工前把小的送下楼（其中0.15克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +got_factory_creche_place; role; knowledge “the factory creche takes only night-shift mothers”_

- **1942.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig嫌Mitzi的床单滴水滴到Hedwig家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Mitzi下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Mitzi把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Hedwig那侧。
  _effects: cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

  Friedrich Winter在巷口拦住Mitzi，说只要确认Hermann Weiss的话，下个月配给多一份。Mitzi看见Hermann Weiss站在对面屋檐下，手里也提着一只空篮子。Mitzi说“我没看见”，Friedrich Winter把本子合上，说“想清楚”。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +refused_to_confirm_informer; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000134:child; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; knowledge “the gendarme offered extra rations for confirmation”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

  _effects: flag +denounced_someone; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000134:child; condition; cash 2 (配合调查后领到的补助); knowledge “告发者的名字不会写进邻居的案卷，但警察会记得是谁递的纸”_

  Mitzi把木架上的《新自由报》从头版读到讣告栏，中间续了两次水，第三杯 Melange 端上来时奶泡已经塌了，三杯一共六十赫勒。侍者把找零的铜板拍在桌上，没有催的意思；Mitzi折好报纸夹回木架，记住了议会辩论里的两个名字，出门时雨还没停。

- **1944.09** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.flea-market-cup/flea-market-cup.haggle-cheap` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, flea_market, object
  摊主是个戴眼镜的老头，开价时把杯子往摊布上一顿。Mitzi指出杯口有个磕痕，Emil Weiss 3又添了一句裂纹渗水。老头摘下眼镜擦了擦，说三十就三十，反正搁着也是搁着。她数出三枚十赫勒的硬币，心里算着比杂货铺的新杯子还是便宜。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (还价后买下旧杯子); skill_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/postwar-illness` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  Mitzi在战后第二个月病倒，医院没有足够的药，医生只给了退烧粉。邻居每天送来一碗汤，放在门口就走。
  _effects: cash -4 (medicine); condition_

- **1945.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/enrolls` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi带着Gustav Bauer去学校登记。孩子的名字被写在名册最后一行，墨水还没有干；回家的路上，Gustav Bauer一直捏着那张课程表，Mitzi则在算这个月还能剩下多少煤钱。

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001979:child; reputation -0.04; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1945.01** `r13-fam.arrange-childcare::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/childcare-arranged` tags: task, care, childcare, work, neighbor, wages, vienna, urban
  Mitzi天没亮就把Gustav Bauer裹好，抱到楼下Elisabeth Stein家。Elisabeth Stein接过孩子，先摸了摸尿布，又指给Mitzi看灶上温着的米汤。Mitzi把一星期的工钱数出一份放在柜上，Elisabeth Stein数也没数就收进围裙；出门时，Mitzi听见孩子在屋里哭了一声，又停了。
  _effects: cash -2 (上工前把小的送下楼（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +arranged_daily_childcare; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; knowledge “the minder keeps a pot of rice water on the stove”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1945.06** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sibling-medicine-bill/advance-with-room-claim` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Mitzi把十四克朗的整张票子压在药费单上，说这钱不用还，只要求母亲那间朝院子的屋子以后归自己照看，钥匙也归自己管。Franz Lechner的咖啡杯在桌上顿了一下，说照看可以，房契上的名字另说；药费单总算从糖罐底下抽了出来，兄妹之间却多了一层账。
  _effects: cash -14 (先垫付母亲全部药费（其中5克朗记入债务）); flag +claims_mother_room_

- **1946.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/half-scholarship` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, vienna, urban, postwar
  校长看了Gustav Bauer的算术本，说可以免掉一半学费。Mitzi在办公室里坐了很久，反复确认“不是借的”；出来时，Gustav Bauer正在操场上追一只皮球，鞋底已经磨穿了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 38; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 32; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 65; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled true -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash 2 (the first instalment of the school grant); reputation 0.029999999999999992_

- **1946.01** `r13-civ.help-refugee::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sheltered-two-nights` tags: social, refugee, shelter, war, trust, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton Bauer站在走廊尽头，脚边一只用绳子捆住的纸箱，问能不能借宿两晚。Mitzi把厨房的折叠床打开，又烧了一壶水。夜里，Anton Bauer在梦里说了一种她听不懂的话，Mitzi把门闩又检查了一遍。
  _effects: cash -2 (走廊里的陌生口音（其中2克朗记入债务）); food; flag +sheltered_refugee_two_nights; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001460:child_

- **1946.02** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/quarrel-with-clerk` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Mitzi和门房为号码顺序争起来，被推到街边，帽子滚进水沟。傍晚回来时窗口已经关了，长椅上空空荡荡，只剩一张踩脏的挂号单；Mitzi在湿冷街头耗了一天，咳嗽更深，诊所门口的人也都记住了这张吵红的脸。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1946.06** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  第一趟过街口时车上的煤箱差点翻下来，Emil Berg用膝盖顶住，Mitzi把绳子又收了一道。第三趟回来，两人靠在门框上喘气，鞋底都是灰，省下的钱还揣在围裙里。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002241:child_

  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer_

- **1946.11** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 32 -> 35; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -4 (被征用的粮食折价（其中4克朗记入债务）); flag +requisition_receipt; knowledge “征用收据只在下一批检查前有效。”_

  Mitzi点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Rosa Kral量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); health -0.04 (通宵缝纫，眼睛疲劳，手指扎伤); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122_

  把孩子送进教室不到一个钟头，Gustav Bauer就从后门跑了出来，鞋掉了一只。Mitzi在操场边追上，孩子说老师讲的字像蚂蚁，坐不住。Mitzi没有骂，只把孩子背回家，一路上听见教室里传出整齐的读书声。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +child_ran_from_school; condition_

- **1947.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi没有立刻答话，先去敲Helene Berg 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Helene Berg 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Mitzi把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

  钳子夹住牙根时，Mitzi听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Anna Weiss 2扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用（其中3克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs（其中0.7克朗记入债务）); health -0.03 (crowded sleeping space); condition; mood -0.02000000000000013; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001979:child_

  _effects: cash -2 (通行证手续费（其中2克朗记入债务）); knowledge “城西修配厂只认盖蓝章的通行证。”_

  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi刚开口，Franz Lechner就把报纸举高，说这张桌子有人。Mitzi端着杯子在门口站了一会儿，回去把椅子放正；第二天同一时辰，Franz Lechner没有来，桌上留着一圈没擦干的杯印。

  Emil Huber不许圣像离开教堂，Mitzi就把邻人请到自家谷仓，在压葡萄的木桶上摆了一盏灯。二十几个人挤在干草里念经，Katharina Weiss站在门口没有进来，只把一篮苹果放在门槛上。
  _effects: condition; flag +held_saints_prayer_in_barn; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1948.10** `r3-mig.xenophobic-landlord::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/landlord-called-police` tags: migration, housing, hostility, landlord, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi拒绝加租，Emil Hofer 2第二天带着警察来，说屋里有六个人登记，实际住了九个。Anton Bauer站在楼梯口，说愿意替Mitzi作证只住了四个。警察数了数床垫，把数字写在本子上，房东在旁边补充“他们晚上还烧煤油炉”。
  _effects: condition; flag +landlord_reported_overcrowding; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001460:child; knowledge “the landlord counts mattresses for the police”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120_

  面包房后门的泔水桶边，Mitzi捡起半块没沾灰的黑面包，掰成两半。Rosa把大的那半推回去，说自己早上吃过；其实Rosa的胃从昨夜就在叫。两人蹲在台阶上吃完，约好谁也不许把这件事说出去。多年后Rosa仍记得，那半块面包边上有股煤灰味。

  Otto Bauer 2让全班按座位排队，Maria Weiss在讲台边摆开酒精瓶和针盒。轮到Rosa时，Otto Bauer 2替她把袖子卷好，说别怕，去年他自己也种过。下课后，黑板上还留着一行没擦掉的日期。

  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); food_

- **1931.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Anton Lechner从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1931.07** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa和Franz Winter谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Rosa家，钥匙只在白天给。Franz Winter说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Franz Bauer听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Rosa把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

  _effects: knowledge “Schnitzler 的剧作在 Burgtheater 上演时，观众会把剧情与维也纳的医生、官员和审查制度对照着议论。”; relation acquaintance with person-arthur-schnitzler; cash -3 (gallery ticket and coat check); mood 0.020000000000000018_

  巡逻队把那个收破烂的老人按在墙上，说他半夜撬门。Rosa看见老人手里只有一根铁钩，想上去拦，Grete Stein拉住她的袖子，说“别管”。Hermann Bauer的警棍落下去时，马灯晃了一下，墙上的影子也跟着晃。第二天，老人没有再来这条街。

- **1936.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Hedwig抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Rosa把床单对折，腾出一半，Hedwig用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005056_

- **1936.01** `r13-fam.separate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::separation-crisis/forced-to-stay` tags: task, family, separation, marriage, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  失业的人挤满了出租屋，Rosa去问了六家，没有一家肯租单间。Franz Hofer把自己的衣服收进一只箱子，却找不到地方放；最后箱子又塞回床底。两人把床用一块旧帘子隔开，夜里谁翻身，帘子都跟着动。
  _effects: cash -2 (把铺盖搬到走廊尽头（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +separated_under_one_roof; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; knowledge “no landlord will rent a single room during the crisis”_

- **1936.03** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/reply-at-bakery` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  第四天，Franz Hofer的回信从门缝底下塞进来，纸边被门槛磨毛了。Franz Hofer约Rosa星期六早上在街角面包店门口见，说想买一个两赫勒的小圆面包，两个人分。Anton Winter在楼道里撞见Rosa读信，笑着问是不是好消息；Rosa把信折回四折，塞进内袋贴着胸口。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood 0.07000000000000006; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1936.04** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa把通知念给Anton Huber 2听，Anton Huber 2摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Rosa去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1936.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Rosa在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Alois Hofer。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Franz Hofer把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Rosa躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.08 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:117_

- **1937.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Rosa在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Rosa把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。

  Rosa把纸条压在糖罐下面，算了半天：两克朗正好是四天的面包钱。月底还是把涨后的房租数出来交给看门人，铜币在桌上摞成两小堆。那一周的晚饭改成面包和稀汤，猪油省着抹。
  _effects: cash -2 (按涨租纸条多付的两克朗（其中2克朗记入债务）); food_

  Rosa把孩子的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。孩子今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Rosa听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: move place-wien; cash -5 (反向车票和电话费（其中5克朗记入债务）); flag +border_refused; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; relation kin with person-franz-lechner; condition; health -0.020000000000000004 (在边境站过夜); knowledge “没有固定住址会被拒绝入境，亲戚的电话不能作为住址证明”_

  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Emil Kral把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Rosa去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05000000000000001 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

  Rosa把银餐具裹进围裙，塞给Alois Lechner，说“先放你家煤窖”。Emil Bauer 3来查时，只看见空木盒和一张旧照片。Alois Lechner晚上把围裙送回来，说“煤窖潮，银器会黑”。Rosa把围裙藏在床垫下面，一夜没睡。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +hid_silver_in_neighbors_cellar; relation friend with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the neighbor’s coal cellar is never inspected”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

  药房在街口，柜台玻璃下面压着价目纸。Rosa把两克朗推过去，伙计找回来十二赫勒，药片用报纸包成一个小包。工友接过去时手还在抖，报纸边角很快被汗洇软。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

  _effects: cash 1 (周末帮工的工钱); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; flag +weekend_shop_work_

  _effects: skill; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  Rosa没有再修钟，把上工的时间交给楼下教堂的钟声。风从多瑙河那边刮过来时，钟声就散，窗户一关更听不见。有一回她误了钟点，到车间时工头已经在门口站着，扣了半个克朗。

  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -5 (temporary shelter（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

  _effects: cash 2.8 (卖旧家具所得); mood -0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个（其中0.08克朗记入债务）)_

  Rosa把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Anna Moser 2借给Rosa一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Rosa睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; mood -0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

  Rosa把帽子捏在手里上台，说救济表格要填三张、盖章两处，煤却今晚就见底。年轻人拍椅子背，Friedrich Berg在门口皱着眉。主持人记下赞成与反对的人数，纸板上的字越写越小。

  _effects: skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +observed_full_shiva_and_lost_job; knowledge “the employer counts mourning days as unauthorized absence”; unemployment (dismissed for observing the seven days of mourning); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +kept_district_list_private; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +hidden_grain; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  _effects: reputation 0.01999999999999999_

  Grete Bauer把Rosa的袖子捏在手里，先数篮子里的面包，再数口袋里的硬币。Friedrich Moser站在门口说“少了两只”，Luise Kral却记得货架上本来就空着。Grete Bauer把一张蓝色传票塞进Rosa手里，说下周三上午九点，别迟到。

  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.04; condition_

- **1920.05** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Alois Weiss从讲台抽屉里拿出一本没有封面的书，书脊用线重新缝过。Emil答应只在课间看。第三天放学下起雨，书角在书包里洇湿。Emil回家用熨斗隔着旧布压了半晚，还书时把淋湿的那页也指给Alois Weiss看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Anna Bauer把Emil拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Emil把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Emil蹲下把两包并排，按Anna Bauer说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Emil站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Karl Kraus在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Emil掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Emil才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus_

- **1922.09** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳下挂着湿床单，孩子们绕着跑，鞋底把水甩到墙上。Emil在第三圈超过Franz Berg，手先碰到墙根。两颗玻璃弹珠进了Emil口袋，外套第二颗纽扣却崩进下水道。Franz Berg从二楼窗口骂了一句，扔下一根别针。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  巷口的喊声第二次传来时，Emil正用膝盖压住木盆边沿，给Luise Bauer挡穿堂风。Luise Bauer在旧布底下翻了个身，Emil立刻停住，等呼吸又匀了才敢动。Josef Bauer推门进来，先看见盆里睡着的Luise Bauer，再看见Emil冻红的脚背，把灶上留的汤热了热。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000553:child; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1923.12** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店门口的石阶上摆着一排烟画。Emil把三张皱巴巴的卡摊开，Anna Weiss挑了挑，从口袋里摸出一张皇帝像。交换完，Anna Weiss转身进了杂货铺，把皇帝像往柜台上一拍，换了一块糖。Emil站在门外，手里还留着卡片的油墨味。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000257:child_

- **1923.12** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Emil把书压在算术本底下，只露出书脊。Anna Bauer走过来，抽走算术本，书也跟着滑出来。折角那页画着一艘帆船。Anna Bauer合上书，说放学后擦黑板，擦一星期；书被锁进讲台抽屉，没往校长室送。
  _effects: mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +wish:love:pursuing_

- **1924.10** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Emil把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Josef Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Emil那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1924.12** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/quarrel-who-leaves` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition, injury
  车票只够一个人走，晚饭桌上话越说越大声。Anna Bauer说该自己走，Emil说自己也快够岁数了。母亲把汤勺往锅沿一磕，屋里静下来，最后Emil摔门出去在楼梯上坐了半宿，直到楼上有人喊冷。
  _effects: relation kin with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.07; health -0.030000000000000027 (在冰冷的楼梯上坐到半夜)_

- **1925.01** `r2-faith.parish-school-fee::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/free-place-granted` tags: faith, school, charity, education, fees, reform, medical, schooling
  Helene Winter在学费簿上找到Emil的名字，用红笔在旁边写了“免”。Grete Stein说课本还得自己买，Helene Winter就从柜子里翻出一本旧算术，封面写着上一个学生的名字。Emil把书抱回家，Josef Bauer用报纸包了书皮，说念完这本再说。
  _effects: flag +received_free_parish_school_place; role; relation friend with person-grete-stein; knowledge “the parish keeps a red-ink list of fee exemptions”; education primary_

  Emil把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Emil就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Emil还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Emil在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (考前最后一夜的煤油); mood 0.06; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

  Maria Kral把Emil生父的旧大衣拿去当铺，说家里不缺这件碍事的衣服。Emil追到巷口，把当票抢回来，撕成两半。Josef Bauer站在两人中间，最后谁也没有帮。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999999; condition; flag +conflict_over_dead_fathers_coat; relation rival with person-maria-kral; relation rival with person-josef-bauer_

  Grete Stein把Emil的名字从升学名单上划掉，说厂里正缺人手。家里没有人争，只把午饭里的半块面包塞给Emil。Emil进了纺织厂，工牌上写着“临时”，每天站十二个钟头，月底工钱比学徒多两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); health -0.06000000000000005 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把Emil分到装订间，每天站十个小时，工钱按件算。第一周结束，Emil的指关节肿了，工头说习惯就好。
  _effects: employment Buchbinderhilfe @ org-winter-print; health -0.040000000000000036 (standing all day); condition_

  _effects: cash -0.1 (只交了一个月会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; flag +workers_youth_quit; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

  成绩单上差两分。Otto Bauer 2说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Josef Bauer来签字。Josef Bauer在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Emil把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字); mood -0.15000000000000002; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-josef-bauer_

- **1926.08** `vienna-youth-threshold.school-leaving/one-more-year-commercial` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Emil把离校证明推回Otto Bauer 2的桌面，说想再读一年，考商业学校。Otto Bauer 2翻出旧课本和一张油印的考试科目表，书费要两克朗四十赫勒。家里那口锅还得照常烧，这笔钱最终是从母亲的首饰盒底抠出来的。
  _effects: cash -2.4 (商业学校考试用的旧课本与报名费); education primary; mood 0.04999999999999999_

  _effects: cash -0.8 (病假扣一天工钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  _effects: cash 1 (同一把刨子); flag +worked_up_from_dogsbody; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +refused_follower; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; knowledge “the soldier left a letter under the cellar door”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1929.09** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne先看Emil的手，又让Emil推两下刨子试试。Emil用力过猛，刨刀啃进木头，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne皱眉，让再来一遍。第二遍木花薄而匀。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说“留下”，当面点了三克朗给Josef Bauer，算头一周饭钱，又指了指墙上的钟，说迟到一刻钟扣半天。
