  Grete Stein把话说得很慢：母亲还在，弟弟的学徒期还有三年。Emil点头，把准备好的结婚证词折起来放回口袋，那是借教堂的旧纸写的。两人在电车站分开，约好明年复活节再谈。Grete Stein上车前替母亲拢了拢披肩，没有回头看。
  _effects: relation friend with person-grete-stein; flag +engagement_postponed; cash -1 (tram fare to the meeting)_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Grete Stein的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1930.01** `r13-fam.mediate-sibling-conflict::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/truce` tags: task, family, sibling, conflict, mediation, reform, medical, schooling
  Elisabeth Bauer把Klara的帽子扔进院子，Klara又把晾衣杆撂倒。Emil站在两人中间，先把帽子捡起来拍干净，再让两人各说一遍是谁先动的手。说到第三遍，两人都不那么肯定了；Emil把帽子放在桌上，说谁再扔就赔谁的工钱。
  _effects: flag +mediated_sibling_fight; role; skill; relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer_

- **1930.04** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Rosa把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Emil把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Rosa低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; flag +rent_increase_accepted; health -0.020000000000000018 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase)_

- **1930.08** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Emil跑了一趟医院，把Josef Bauer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Emil回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); health 0.05999999999999999 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +parent_hospitalized_

- **1931.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household
  Grete Stein在几天后找到Emil，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。
  _effects: relation partner with person-grete-stein; household; role; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003170_

- **1931.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Emil把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Emil读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1931.01** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  账本的纸边已经发毛，Emil翻到那一页时，手指沾上一层灰。有人说“三十四克朗够买两袋面粉”，没人接话。最后举手表决，赞成销账的手比反对的多两只，老成员把手放下去时，椅子又晃了一下。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; flag +union_debt_written_off_

- **1931.01** `task-vienna-bourgeois-youth.concert-outing/church-concert` tags: bourgeois, education, culture, salon, university, family, task, task:culture
  Emil算了一下，四克朗够一个星期午饭。他改约Grete Kral 2去教堂听免费的管风琴音乐会，捐了两赫勒，坐在后排的长凳上。回去时两人在街角分吃一个烤土豆。
  _effects: cash -0.06 (教堂捐献与烤土豆); food; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1931.10** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Emil把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Maria Weiss替Emil扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1931.12** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Emil在楼梯间守夜时，邻居说起楼下老太太病重。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/dismissed-after-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, unemployment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Emil不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Emil拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Emil的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); health -0.12 (injury and lost job); cash -6 (medical treatment)_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-teaching` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Emil的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Emil被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。
  _effects: cash -4 (reduced fee for a teaching-house call); health 0.030000000000000027 (examined by a teaching team); condition; role; knowledge “教学出诊会有实习医生和学生轮流听诊，最后请上级定方案，药费可以减半。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1932.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Franz Lechner说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Franz Lechner陪着Emil去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-franz-lechner; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1932.11** `statistical_infection`
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1932.12** `r2-leisure.postcard-cipher::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/reported-the-postcard` tags: leisure, postcard, letters, secrecy, surveillance, reform, medical, schooling
  Maria Weiss把明信片翻来覆去看了两遍，说这张得交给邮局。Emil说只是写错了季节，Maria Weiss说写错季节的人不会把“勿念”写得这样重。第二天，Gustav Berg来敲门，问Emil Bauer 2是谁，住在哪里。Emil说不知道，明信片是寄给这栋楼的。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; reputation -0.04; condition; flag +reported_suspicious_postcard; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; knowledge “the landlady reports unusual postcards to the post office”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1932.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Emil的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Grete Stein把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Emil留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Emil在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-grete-stein; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods); mood -0.06000000000000005; role_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004261_

  Emil把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Lena没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Emil回头看了一眼，Lena正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1933.01** `r13-fam.help-neighbour::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/help-refused` tags: task, social, neighbor, help, care, reform, medical, schooling
  Ferdinand Winter把煤筐往身后挪，说不用，自己搬得动。Emil站在楼梯转角，听见对方上一层歇一层，筐底在墙上刮出长长的白印。晚饭时，Emil把自家的汤盛了一碗，放在Ferdinand Winter门口，没有敲门。
  _effects: flag +left_soup_at_neighbour_door; relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter_

- **1933.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Emil的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1933.05** `statistical_recovery`
  Emil熬过了这场肺结核，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1933.08** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Alois Huber把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Alois Huber在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1933.09** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.church-bell` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Emil没有再修钟，把上工的时间交给楼下教堂的钟声。风从多瑙河那边刮过来时，钟声就散，窗户一关更听不见。有一回她误了钟点，到车间时工头已经在门口站着，扣了半个克朗。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (迟到被扣工钱)_

  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Rosa的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。

  腌鲱鱼切成两段，一段今天吃，一段留着。黑面包硬得要用刀锯。Franz Huber来借盐，眼睛落在鱼上，说可以用半棵卷心菜换一块。Emil切了一指宽递过去。
  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000441:child_

- **1937.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Rosa的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

  Emil把面粉撒在桌上抹平，用指头写了一个“M”，让Josef Bauer照着画。Josef Bauer的手指粗得像胡萝卜，第一笔就戳穿了面层；Emil又抹平一次，这次握着老人的手腕慢慢走。写到自己的名字时，Josef Bauer忽然笑了，说原来这几个弯是这个意思。
  _effects: flag +taught_parent_to_write_name; role; skill; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; skill_

- **1937.08** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Alois Lechner凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Emil把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1937.08** `r1-cul.diary-discovery::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/did-not-open-it` tags: culture, diary, family, privacy, secrets, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil没有翻开日记，把它放回床垫下，又压了一只旧鞋在上面。晚饭时Klara一直看Emil，Josef Bauer问两人是不是吵架了；Emil说没有，Klara低头把汤喝完，碗底剩下一块胡萝卜。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +did_not_read_diary; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “the sibling noticed the diary had been moved”_

  Emil把通知念给Berta Berg 3听，Berta Berg 3摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Emil去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1938.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction
  Rosa没有答应，只说现在不合适。Emil把这件事收回去，之后见面仍点头。

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005808_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Emil在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Gustav Berg记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Emil带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

- **1938.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.box-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Emil咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Emil手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Ferdinand Bauer等在门口，问值不值，Emil只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价); mood 0.07999999999999996; reputation 0.019999999999999997; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94_

- **1938.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Grete Stein在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Grete Stein敲开Emil的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-grete-stein; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1938.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Emil就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Hermann Bauer摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1938.09** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil和Emil Bauer 2谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Emil家，钥匙只在白天给。Emil Bauer 2说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Gustav Berg听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Emil把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Gustav Berg把征用单按在柜台上，Emil家里的两袋面粉被划走一袋，换回一张盖蓝章的收据。Gustav Berg说下周还会来，如果地窖里再翻出粮食，收据就不作数了。
  _effects: cash -4 (被征用的粮食折价); food; flag +requisition_receipt; knowledge “征用收据只在下一批检查前有效。”_

- **1939.01** `kernel-social.lending::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::lending-crisis/lending.sewing-machine` tags: task, task:social, social, lending, debt, neighbor, money, everyday
  Emil的房租也到期了，只好把Alois Lechner的缝纫机搬到自己屋里，说等还钱再还机器。Alois Lechner来取针线盒时，看见缝纫机头上盖着布，站了一会儿就走了；第二天，整栋楼都知道这件事。
  _effects: relation rival with person-alois-lechner; role; reputation -0.04; flag +seized_sewing_machine; knowledge “缝纫机被扣作抵押，针线盒还留在邻居手里。”_

- **1939.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/leave-before-dessert` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  问到后来，Josef Bauer提起别家有个更稳当的差事。Franz Hofer把餐巾往桌上一放，说吃好了，先走。两人在楼梯间站住，Franz Hofer说不是Emil的错，可下次再来还得一样坐这张桌子；Emil心里松了一点，也知道这顿饭没吃完，事情并没有过去。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Franz Bauer把图纸摊在窗口，用尺量了后院到邻居墙的距离，说退让不够，要改。Emil说改了就少一间房，Franz Bauer说少一间房总比拆了好。Ferdinand Bauer在旁边重新画了一条线，把厨房往后挪了半米；Franz Bauer收下修改图，盖了三个章，说脚手架搭起来之前还要来验一次。
  _effects: cash -18 (脚手架上的批文); flag +building_permit_granted_after_setback; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; knowledge “the kitchen must be moved half a meter from the boundary”_

- **1939.10** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/shelter` tags: war, homefront, shortage
  警报响时Emil躲进地下室，和十几户人挤在长凳上。头顶传来闷响，灰尘从梁上落下。警报解除后，街上多了一堆碎瓦。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1940.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::pharmacy-war-requisition/pharmacy-war-morphine-trade` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, crisis
  军需处先领走了奎宁和碘酒，药房只剩阿司匹林和泻盐。Emil用一双旧靴子换了两支吗啡，药剂师把安瓿裹在报纸里，叮嘱别在街上打开。他把安瓿藏进帽衬，过检查站时低着头。
  _effects: cash -20 (boots and cash traded for two morphine ampoules); health 0.019999999999999997 (pain relief from a black-market ampoule); role; flag +traded_for_morphine; knowledge “军需处先领走奎宁和碘酒，药房只剩阿司匹林；旧靴子可以换两支吗啡。”_

  家里那间屋子朝北，下午的光只够照亮桌角。Emil把车钱塞进外套内袋，听见母亲在数硬币时嘴里念念有词。师兄说过别到处讲，Emil就没讲，连妹妹问起也只说“够用”。
  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111_

  _effects: flag +reported_shark_lender; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; knowledge “police treat the shark loan as a civil matter”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

  _effects: cash -3 (收养登记处的空栏); flag +adoption_registered; role; relation guardian with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002539:child; knowledge “the adoption form requires the birth father’s death certific”_

  Luise Bauer在城里站稳了脚，托人带来一个信封，里面是八克朗和一张夜校报名单。信上只写：当年你教我算术，现在轮到我付学费。Emil把报名单看了三遍，填好，第二天一早就去交了。

  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

  洗衣房里的人压低声音说，Franz Lechner已经把Emil的名字写进了门房的本子，说Emil替罢工的人送过饭。Emil没有吵，只问本子现在在谁手里。Anton把最后一件衣服拧干，说昨晚看见本子被送进了分局。
  _effects: reputation -0.12; flag +named_in_porter_book; relation rival with person-franz-lechner; knowledge “the porter’s book was taken to the district police office”_

  城门口的旗换了颜色，岗哨开始检查每一张纸。Emil回家时发现街角新钉了一块告示牌：天黑以后不许出门，地窖里的粮食要登记。晚上，巡逻队的靴声从窗下经过，比钟声还准。
  _effects: state_add occupied-city-regime state:world-vienna-1912:169; mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +saw_the_occupation_flag; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

  星期天中午，Emil和Franz Hofer在出租屋的厨房桌边分面包，话题从房租转到Emil母亲上个月的药钱。Franz Hofer把刀放下，说这个家不能总先填那边的窟窿。面包凉了，汤也没再热。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1941.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef Weiss嫌Emil的床单滴水滴到Josef Weiss家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Emil下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Emil把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Josef Weiss那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

  Emil把传单读了两遍，记下上面的地址，却没有去。第二天照常上工；午休时听见两个工友低声谈论同一场集会。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1942.01** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/name-the-neighbor` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Emil告诉Ferdinand Stein，Luise Berg 2家的地窖里还有两桶猪油。征用队下午就去了那户人家。Emil拿到一小袋盐和一张优先配给卡，回家时没有走平时那条街。
  _effects: cash 1 (检举邻居得到的配给奖励); reputation 0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; flag +denounced_neighbor_for_grain; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1942.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::pharmacy-war-requisition/pharmacy-war-flour-powder` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, crisis
  药房的后门开着，一个穿军大衣的人按包卖药：退烧粉、止血粉、止痛粉。Emil买了三包，回家发现其中一包是面粉；再去找人，后门已经锁了。他把那包面粉倒进碗里，又倒回纸袋，没舍得扔。
  _effects: cash -15 (three packets bought from a man in an army coat); condition; knowledge “后门按包卖的药可能是面粉；再回去找人，门已经锁了。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

- **1942.04** `r1-fam.missing-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/missing.unresolved` tags: family, missing, migration, letters, grief, vienna, urban, crisis
  Friedrich Winter说没有死亡证明，就不能算死了，也不能领任何东西。Emil拿回一张盖了“查无此人”的纸，在邮局门口站了一会儿。回家后，他把弟弟的房间收拾干净，床单换了新的；母亲问是不是有消息了，他说还没有。
  _effects: cash -2 (邮局没有回音); mood -0.10000000000000009; flag +missing_relative_unresolved; role; knowledge “没有死亡证明，家里什么也领不到。”_

- **1942.11** `r1-civ.return-from-exile::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/returned-to-occupied-flat` tags: migration, exile, return, papers, home, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil回到旧住处，门锁换了，开门的是陌生人。Franz Hofer从楼下上来，说Emil的母亲三年前搬走了，房子被分给了别人。Emil在楼梯上坐了一会儿，闻见楼道里还是那股煤和酸菜的味道，才敢确定自己真的回来了。
  _effects: cash -2 (回程车票上的旧名字); condition; flag +returned_to_occupied_flat; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; knowledge “the old flat was reassigned while the subject was abroad”_

- **1943.01** `r13-work.practice-craft::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/split-good-wood` tags: work, craft, practice, wood, skill, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil练到半夜，刨刃吃得太深，一块好料从中间裂开。木料钱要从下周的菜钱里出，Grete Weiss听见响声，隔着墙问是不是在劈柴。Emil把裂开的板子翻过来，看见木纹里有一道旧钉眼，才明白这块料本来就不该用。
  _effects: cash -2 (第四十个榫头); flag +work13_split_good_wood; skill_

- **1943.05** `r3-mig.emigration-agency::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/deposit-vanished` tags: migration, papers, travel, debt, fraud, vienna, urban, crisis
  代理把价目表翻到第二页，说船票、行李和“落地介绍”一共四十二克朗。Emil数出十四克朗作定金，Grete Weiss 2在收据上盖了一个没有公司名的圆章。三周后Emil再去，铺面已经换成一家裁缝店，Mitzi说上个月也有人来找过同一个名字。
  _effects: cash -14 (移民代理处的第二张价目表); flag +agency_deposit_lost; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97; knowledge “the emigration agency took deposits under a seal with no com”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97_

- **1943.07** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Franz Bauer追出来问怎么不领了，Emil说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Emil把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; food_

- **1943.10** `kernel-migrate.neighborhood-rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/civic-rumor-verify` tags: migration, rumor, neighbor, work, rural, vienna, urban, crisis
  他没有直接信，先去市场问了两个摊贩，又在报纸中缝找到一条招工启事。启事上的地址和Franz Lechner说的差三条街；他把报纸折好，决定周日自己去看。摊贩说那条街最近换了门牌，旧地址已经找不到。
  _effects: flag +verified_work_rumor; knowledge “招工启事上的地址和邻居说的差三条街，周日可以自己去看”; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; cash -0.5 (买报纸和去市场的车钱); reputation 0.020000000000000004_

- **1943.12** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/hungry-winter` tags: war, homefront, shortage
  Emil用配给卡换到半袋面粉和几根冻土豆。锅里煮的是稀汤，邻居送来一小块人造黄油，说孩子们先吃。
  _effects: food; condition_

- **1944.01** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hand-over-the-grain` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Helene Weiss把征用单按在柜台上，Emil家里的两袋面粉被划走一袋，换回一张盖蓝章的收据。Helene Weiss说下周还会来，如果地窖里再翻出粮食，收据就不作数了。
  _effects: cash -4 (被征用的粮食折价); knowledge “征用收据只在下一批检查前有效。”_

- **1944.01** `r13-fam.care-sick-child::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::child-care-war/milk-watered` tags: task, care, health, child, fever, night, vienna, urban
  配给站给的奶已经掺了水，Emil把它煮开，撇掉上面那层薄薄的沫，还是喂给了Ferdinand Kral。孩子喝一半就推开碗，说苦；Emil尝了一口，才发现锅底糊了。那一夜，Emil把窗子开了又关，怕煤烟，也怕穿堂风。
  _effects: cash -3 (第三遍换湿布); flag +watered_milk_fed; condition_

- **1944.05** `r3-mig.immigrant-neighborhood::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refused-without-two-sponsors` tags: migration, community, organization, mutual_aid, belonging, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil Winter翻到担保人一栏，说必须有两位老会员签字。Alois Bauer 2愿意签一个，另一个名字写了又划掉。Emil Winter把表推回来，说“不是不认老乡，是怕有人借了钱就走”。Emil把一克朗会费收回口袋，听见里屋有人在分汤。
  _effects: flag +refused_mutual_aid_membership; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; knowledge “membership requires two sponsors”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1944.10** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  表格有四栏，第四栏要写“工种及所属行会”。Emil问工友平时怎么称呼自己的活，工友靠在机器旁边说“就是上油”。办事员的印章是木柄的，按下去时咔的一声。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120_

- **1944.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money
  抽屉一推就卡住，Emil用膝盖顶了一下才合严。上工路上经过药房，橱窗里摆着润肺糖浆的广告画，她别过头去。车间主任点名时，她应声比平时慢了一拍。
  _effects: condition_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/displaced` tags: war, liberation, 1945, migration
  炮火把Emil住的那条街烧掉一半。Emil跟着人群走到城外，在临时收容所登记，床位上铺的是发霉的稻草。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -5 (temporary shelter)_

- **1945.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.eyesight-failed` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  排在他前面的Anton Winter 2很快过了关。轮到他时，医生把灯挪近了些，他还是念错。出了南站，太阳才刚爬上车皮，他站在路边把揉皱的证明捋平。
  _effects: flag +seeking_work_

- **1945.05** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Lena把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Emil把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Lena低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1946.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-aspirin-substitute` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, postwar
  药房说没有货，Emil把处方叠好，去了下一家。天黑时手里只有一包阿司匹林和一瓶咳嗽糖浆；Luise Kral 3说总比空手强，把糖浆让给了孩子。回家后他把阿司匹林分成两半，糖浆瓶口用纸塞着，漏了一点。
  _effects: cash -8 (aspirin and cough syrup after a day of searching); health -0.03 (the prescribed medicine was not found); condition; role_

- **1946.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Anton Huber 2把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Emil去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1946.06** `vienna-illness-household.wage-earner-injury/take-to-hospital` tags: health, family, home, injury
  Anton Winter 3在冲床里压了手指，Emil和几个人把Anton Winter 3送到医院。受监管的工人合作社派人送来两克朗，说算工钱里的部分；Anton Winter 3的手指保住了，却再也伸不直，Emil在厂里得了句“够义气”，也知道这名声换不回一根好指头。
  _effects: cash 2 (雇主给的医药补贴); health -0.05 (手指压伤留下残疾); reputation 0.05; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1946.09** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Franz Lechner却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Emil把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1947.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Emil没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009939_

  Katharina Weiss把征用单按在柜台上，Emil家里的两袋面粉被划走一袋，换回一张盖蓝章的收据。Katharina Weiss说下周还会来，如果地窖里再翻出粮食，收据就不作数了。
  _effects: cash -4 (被征用的粮食折价); food; knowledge “征用收据只在下一批检查前有效。”_

- **1947.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.give-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Emil只喝了半杯就看见门口站着等位的老主顾，手里转着帽子。Emil把报纸叠好放回木架，起身让出窗边的位子，一杯 Melange 三十赫勒，侍者点头致意，没再收第二杯的钱。走出门时雨小了些，Emil站在檐下理了理衣领，觉得这半个下午也不算白来。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (一杯 Melange 的价钱); reputation 0.03; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.02** `r13-rel.old-friend-returns::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/kept-him-at-the-door` tags: relationship, friend, return, memory, obligation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil请Luise Berg 2在门口等了一会儿，说屋里乱。Luise Berg 2站着没动，把帽子在手里转了两圈。两人说了几句天气，Luise Berg 2就说还要去看别人。Emil关上门，从窗子里看见Luise Berg 2在巷口回头了一次。
  _effects: flag +kept_distance; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1947.07** `vienna-adult-social.sunday-table/sunday-table.argument` tags: adulthood, social, work, family, partnership
  星期天中午，Emil和Franz Hofer在出租屋的厨房桌边分面包，话题从房租转到Emil母亲上个月的药钱。Franz Hofer把刀放下，说这个家不能总先填那边的窟窿。面包凉了，汤也没再热。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1947.07** `statistical_infection`
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1947.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Emil没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1947.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Emil的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1947.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Emil的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1948.01** `statistical_recovery`
  Emil熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/waiting-list` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  Emil去住房局问结果，办事员翻过卡片说分数不够：家里人口少，现住房虽挤但还没到拆除标准。回程的电车上，Ferdinand 2劝他明年再递一次申请。
  _effects: role; cash -2 (application and tram fare); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `r13-fam.return-hometown::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::hometown-postwar/family-gone` tags: task, family, return, hometown, migration, relative, vienna, urban
  Emil回到老街区，老屋的屋顶塌了一角，院子里长满荨麻。Anna Bauer从城外赶来，说家里的人逃的逃、散的散，只剩自己还认得这块地。两人在门槛上坐了一会儿，Emil把箱子打开，里面是给每个人带的礼物，已经不知道该给谁。
  _effects: cash -2 (火车进站时看见旧钟楼); condition; flag +returned_to_an_empty_home; relation kin with person-anna-bauer; knowledge “most of the family scattered during the war”_

- **1948.04** `r3-war.veterans-hall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/joined-veterans-association` tags: war, veterans, association, organization, postwar, vienna, urban, reform
  Rosa把名册摊在酒馆后屋，让Emil在“伤兵”和“未伤”两栏里选。Emil说自己在战壕里冻掉两个脚趾，Rosa说那不算伤；Grete Huber在旁边敲了敲杯子，说“算不算，先喝一杯”。
  _effects: flag +joined_veterans_association; role; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001448:child; organization_membership; knowledge “the association separates wounded from unwounded”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108_

- **1948.04** `r1-civ.municipal-housing-list::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::r1-civ.municipal-housing-list-reform/reform-points-appeal` tags: housing, reform, bureaucracy, tenement, postwar, vienna, urban, scarcity
  名单公布时，Emil发现比自己积分低的两户排在了前面，其中一户是区议员的亲戚。Friedrich Winter说可以申诉，但要先交一笔“复核费”；Emil把申诉书交上去，三个月没有回音，最后搬进了岳母家的厨房。
  _effects: cash -2 (新住宅名单上的门牌); condition; flag +housing_points_appeal_ignored; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000134:child; knowledge “the review fee is paid before the appeal is read”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

- **1948.08** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Emil跑了一趟医院，把Josef Bauer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Emil回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); health 0.05999999999999999 (父亲住进医院得到治疗)_

- **1949.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Grete Stein攥着Emil的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-grete-stein_

- **1949.01** `r13-work.inherit-business::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/debts-came-with-it` tags: commerce, inheritance, family_firm, debt, succession, vienna, urban, postwar
  钥匙交到手里第二天，供货商就拿着欠条上门。Josef Bauer说那些账是铺子的，不是他的；供货商说铺子现在归Emil，账也归Emil。Emil把存货盘了一遍，能抵的抵，剩下十克朗写了还款计划；印章还在，但盖下去的第一份文件是欠条。
  _effects: cash -10 (父亲的账本和印章); condition; flag +work13_inherited_business_with_debts; role; employment Kaufmann @ org-allgemeines-krankenhaus_

- **1949.02** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Grete Stein排在前面，回头替Emil看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Emil用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.07** `vienna-illness-household.wage-earner-injury/walk-home` tags: health, family, home, injury
  没去医院，Emil送Franz Weiss 2回地下室，布条裹了伤指。妻子端来热水，Emil却惦记着耽误的工时；临走时Franz Weiss 2用另一只手碰了碰Emil的胳膊。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145; mood -0.030000000000000027; health -0.030000000000000027 (伤口只经简单包扎); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145_

  Emil在告解席前跪了很久，最后只说了一句：我不知道还有没有人在听。Helene Weiss没有立刻回答，隔着帘子把一枚旧十字架放在窗台上。Friedrich Bauer在教堂门口等Emil，看见Emil出来时没有划十字，也没有问。
  _effects: condition; flag +confessed_doubt; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000534:child; knowledge “the priest left a worn cross on the window ledge”_

  Emil在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Emil把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011488_

  Katharina Weiss把征用单按在柜台上，Emil家里的两袋面粉被划走一袋，换回一张盖蓝章的收据。Katharina Weiss说下周还会来，如果地窖里再翻出粮食，收据就不作数了。

  埋到第七个时，土里露出上一具尸体的靴子。Grete Stein停了一下，说“换个地方”，Anton Hofer说没地方。Emil把靴子推回土里，继续铲；Gustav Hofer在旁边低声念名字，念错了一个，Grete Stein纠正他，声音很轻。
  _effects: condition; flag +reburied_in_shared_grave; role; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-151; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152_

  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `r13-fam.invite-cafe::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/coffee-accepted` tags: task, social, cafe, friendship, invitation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil在柜台前把两枚硬币捏出汗，才开口请Katharina Kral喝一杯。Katharina Kral挑了靠窗的小桌，把糖块推给Emil，说这里比家里暖和。两人坐到灯亮，杯底的渣子凉透了，Emil还是没有把那句“以后常来”说出口。
  _effects: cash -2 (靠窗那张小桌); mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +bought_a_friend_coffee_

- **1950.07** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  床架从楼梯上拖下来时油漆蹭掉了好几块，Emil站在旁边没作声。收破烂的走后，屋里空得回声都变大了，Emil抱着铁盒出门，只把门锁好，钥匙从看门人手里交出去。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

  人事科的门开了又关，Emil的档案被放在最下面。没有人解释为什么，只有Anton Hofer把一叠旧表格推过来，说“先把这个月做完”。窗外的口号声一阵高一阵低，处里的打字机一直没有停。
  _effects: state_add institution-purge state:world-vienna-1912:245; knowledge “审查名单还没有公开，但人事科已经把所有档案重新排过。”_

  _effects: state_parameter institution-purge.intensity 0.41 -> 0.37; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152; flag +hid_a_name; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1916.06** `p9-childhood-household.supper-bowl/caught-adding-ladle` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  锅盖还冒着气，Lena听见Josefine Hofer去灶口添煤，勺子立刻伸进锅里，刮起粘在锅边的半个土豆。Josefine Hofer回来时没坐下，只站在桌边看。Lena把勺子放回碗里，膝盖并紧，手心在桌沿上蹭出一层汗。
  _effects: mood -0.04; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1917.09** `vienna-early-childhood.fever-night/doctor-bill` tags: childhood, family, home, street

  _effects: cash -1 (医生的诊金); health 0.05999999999999999 (服药后退烧); mood -0.04999999999999999_

  _effects: education primary_

  Lena放学后替Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的排字工送校样，穿过两条街，拿到一克朗。回家时天已经黑了，Josefine Hofer把饭留在锅里；Lena的鞋底磨薄，脚后跟起了泡，却把那一克朗捏得平平整整，放进Josefine Hofer的围裙口袋；这跑腿的差事从此固定下来，Lena也算家里一个能挣钱的孩子。

  Friedrich Huber把一叠“十小时工作制”传单塞进Lena的围裙口袋，叫下班后夹带出厂。Lena把传单塞进鞋垫底下，走到巷口才分给两个女工。第二天Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne搜遍柜子，什么也没找到。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; flag +printshop_union_contact; mood 0.02999999999999997_

  镇上的木匠要一个拉锯的帮手。Lena去了三天，手上磨出水泡，Franz Lechner说再练练。第四天，Lena没有去，跟着Josefine Hofer下了地。木匠后来遇见Josefine Hofer，只说孩子吃不了这碗饭。Lena回家时手上还沾着木屑。
  _effects: education none; skill; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; role; food; flag +left_carpenter_

- **1928.01** `task-vienna-youth-ask-old-worker/lesson-given` tags: task, task:education, work, apprenticeship, learning
  Lena等老工人收拾完字盘，才开口问那道总夹不紧的铅字。Maria Kral 2让他把手放低，示范了三遍，又让他自己做一遍；最后只说“明天再练”。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; knowledge “铅字夹不紧时，先放松手腕再重新上力。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1929.01** `task-vienna-youth-apprenticeship-search/trial-promised` tags: task, task:work, work, adolescence, apprenticeship

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1930.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Lena没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.02999999999999997 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002464_

- **1930.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Lena把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Lena读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Emil Lechner的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

- **1930.01** `r13-edu.scholarship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/full-grant` tags: education, scholarship, stipend, bureaucracy, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena把成绩单和父亲的分期税单一起递进窗口。Gustav Berg翻到家庭收入那栏，用红笔划了一下，让Lena下周三再来。那天Lena从Maria Weiss手里接过蓝印信：每年二百四十克朗，先发二十四。Lena在楼梯口站了很久，才把信折好。那张信纸被Lena压在枕头下，夜里翻身时沙沙作响。
  _effects: cash 12 (助学金窗口的蓝印); flag +scholarship_granted; education higher_

- **1930.07** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.delay` tags: home, market, food, social
  三个克朗拖了九天。玛丽亚收下时数都没数，只把Lena的名字从木牌上抹去，粉笔灰落在卷心菜上。Gustav Berg拎着篮子从旁边过去，没停脚。
  _effects: cash -3 (延迟偿还市场赊账); food; reputation -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

- **1930.07** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Emil Kral点了牛奶咖啡，Lena要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1931.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction
  Emil Lechner没有答应，只说现在不合适。Lena把这件事收回去，之后见面仍点头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003181_

- **1931.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.introduce-two-people/overpraise` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  介绍时把两边都夸过了头，说老友是印厂里最靠得住的人，说女工的手艺整条街都找不出第二个。话太多，两人反倒拘谨起来，各自看窗外。咖啡凉了，坐了不到一个钟头就散了，出门时谁也没提再见面。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和点心); relation friend with person-luise-kral; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  Lena在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Lena去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1936.03** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.wedding-procession/wedding-procession.watch-outside` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, wedding, neighbor, celebration
  新娘的婚纱是租的，裙摆上有一块洗过的痕迹，可她还是走得很小心，像踩在水面上。Lena把粘在袖口的纸花弹掉，听见院里有人喊“新郎官唱一个”，接着是跑调的歌声和一阵拍手。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1936.07** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena把通知念给Emil Huber听，Emil Huber摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Lena去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1937.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Lena在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Lena把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005589_

- **1937.01** `p9-history-union-political.reform-meeting/reform-hand-to-union` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  Lena把请愿书递给工会代表，自己退到后排靠柱子的位置。Anna Bauer从旁边递来一支短铅笔，让Lena把台上说的数目记在请柬背面，回家再誊清楚。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

- **1937.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.give-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Lena只喝了半杯就看见门口站着等位的老主顾，手里转着帽子。Lena把报纸叠好放回木架，起身让出窗边的位子，一杯 Melange 三十赫勒，侍者点头致意，没再收第二杯的钱。走出门时雨小了些，Lena站在檐下理了理衣领，觉得这半个下午也不算白来。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (一杯 Melange 的价钱); reputation 0.03; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.11** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  木盆边的水渍映着天光，Lena跪在石阶上揉搓床单，肘弯发酸。晾绳上的湿布滴答作响，Maria Kral探身把自家被单也挂上去，两人没多说话。
  _effects: health -0.019999999999999997 (长时间浸在碱水里，手部皮肤开裂); relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral_

- **1937.12** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Franz Bauer，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Franz Bauer点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Gustav Berg在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Lena排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

- **1938.01** `r13-civ.press-card::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/card-issued` tags: knowledge, press, credentials, reporter, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil Bauer 3让Lena把帽子摘了，对着墙上的镜头坐直。照片洗出来，Lena的脸被闪光灯照得发白。Otto Bauer 2在表格上签了字，Emil Bauer 3把记者证压进硬纸套，说丢了不补。
  _effects: cash -2 (记者证上的照片); flag +press_card_issued; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; knowledge “a lost press card will not be replaced”_

- **1938.02** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.factory-gate/gate-name-taken` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, factory, hiring
  门房把Lena的名字写进本子，说第二天带身份证件和住址证明来，录不录用还得看Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien的意思。Lena走出几步又回头，问清要几张证明，门房不耐烦地敲了敲玻璃。
  _effects: flag +factory_callback; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1938.06** `hist15-occ.seed-occupation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-old-clerk-stays` tags: occupation, war, curfew, history, place, institution, vienna, urban
  新来的军官站在市政厅门口，旧办事员还在窗后盖章。Lena排队领一张新的通行证，窗口里的人低声说：“名字照旧写，住址别改。”队伍里没有人说话，只有纸张翻动的声音。
  _effects: state_add occupied-city-regime state:world-vienna-1912:137; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “旧办事员仍在窗后盖章，只要名字和住址不改，通行证还能办。”_

- **1938.06** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/second-bridge-promise` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  在第二个桥洞下，Anna Bauer先停下来，用鞋尖碾一颗烤栗子壳，说下星期天还是这个时候、还是这座桥。Lena点头，掏出四赫勒买了两小袋栗子，两人分着剥，手上都是糖渍；桥洞上的水还在滴，谁也没有提万一明天下雨怎么办。
  _effects: relation partner with person-anna-bauer; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子); mood 0.05999999999999994_

- **1939.01** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/hand-over-the-grain` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Franz Bauer把征用单按在柜台上，Lena家里的两袋面粉被划走一袋，换回一张盖蓝章的收据。Franz Bauer说下周还会来，如果地窖里再翻出粮食，收据就不作数了。
  _effects: cash -4 (被征用的粮食折价); food; flag +requisition_receipt; knowledge “征用收据只在下一批检查前有效。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1939.01** `r13-civ.midwife::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/doctor-called` tags: health, midwife, birth, neighbor, night, vienna, urban, crisis
  Grete Stein摸了半天肚子，忽然让Lena去街口叫医生，说胎位不对。Lena披着外套跑过两条街，医生来了以后只说了句“送医院”，又收了一次出诊费。天亮前，马车才把Luise Weiss 2拉走。
  _effects: cash -5 (夜里敲接生婆的门); flag +complicated_birth_sent_to_hospital; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; knowledge “the midwife suspected a malpresentation before dawn”_

- **1939.05** `r3-war.ration-scale::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/exposed-scale-weight` tags: war, ration, shortage, market, protest, vienna, urban, crisis
  配给秤的托盘底下粘着一块铅，Lena把面包放上去时，指针总比别处少半格。Emil Bauer 3说秤是官家的，Lena让他把铅块抠下来；后面排队的人都不说话，只有Otto Bauer 2把自己的面包掰了一半递过来。
  _effects: food; flag +exposed_ration_scale_weight; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97_

- **1939.12** `r3-crime.police-raid::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::political-week/lined-against-the-wall` tags: crime, police, tenement, fear, politics, vienna, urban, crisis
  门是被靴子踹开的，Ferdinand Stein把整层楼的人赶到走廊，让他们面朝墙站好，说“谁动谁先进去”。Helene Weiss的睡帽掉在地上，Friedrich Winter只穿着袜子。抽屉被倒空，床垫被划开，Lena攒了三个月的房租从茶叶罐里消失，没有人说要开收据。
  _effects: cash -7 (凌晨四点门闩被撬开); mood -0.17999999999999994; condition; flag +survived_police_raid; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; knowledge “the raid team took cash without logging it”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1940.01** `r13-work.ask-foreman::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/name-on-the-board` tags: work, hiring, foreman, favour, queue, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil Bauer 3说这个月不添人，让Lena把名字写在门房的纸板上。纸板上已经有四十多个名字，最上面一行被雨泡花了。Lena写得很小，挤在一个“王”字旁边，Emil Bauer 3看了一眼说，回去等吧，别天天来。
  _effects: cash -1 (工头兜里的粉笔头); flag +work13_name_on_the_gate_board; knowledge “the gate board already holds more than forty names”_

- **1940.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  窗外是维也纳第十五区的一条窄街，卖烤栗子的炉子冒着白烟。Lena贴完纸退后两步看，发现“欠”字被浆糊洇开了一点。老成员没说话，只把眼镜摘下来擦了又擦。
  _effects: reputation 0.05000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; flag +union_ledger_public_

- **1940.08** `absurd15-stage.cast-the-king::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/not-king-material` tags: performance, theatre, power, irony, work, pretend, vienna, urban
  Friedrich Huber看了Lena的手，说这双手适合拿账本，不适合拿权杖。Lena回家路上买了一张最便宜的站票，在最后一排看完了整场戏；散场时，Lena记住的不是国王的台词，而是他坐下时椅子发出的声音。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.010000000000000009; cash -1 (买了一张站票); flag +cast_refused; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111_

  鱼头用报纸包着，报纸上还印着昨天的广告。Anton Huber 3一边刮冰一边说，回去多搁姜，别舍不得醋。Lena把纸包拎在手里，能感觉到凉气从指缝里钻出来，走到家时报纸已经洇湿了一层。
  _effects: food; health 0.019999999999999997 (喝上了热鱼汤)_

  Lena在车站长椅上认出Anton，对方正把车票捏在手里。两人隔着一个人的空位坐下，先说了几句天气，Lena才问那年为什么没有来送葬。Anton说车票买了，站台上又折回去；说到一半，两个人都看着铁轨，谁也没有再追问。
  _effects: flag +reconciled_with_estranged_sibling; role; knowledge “the sibling bought a ticket to the funeral but turned back”_

  Lena没有偷书，趁Anton Winter 3去书库，把第三卷的缺页抄了下来。抄到一半，Gustav Berg来查一份旧地图，Anton Winter 3让他登记；Lena把抄本夹进报纸，出门时腿还在抖。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +copied_missing_gazetteer_pages; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; knowledge “the missing pages describe the old market square”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

  Lena把通知念给Josef Berg 2听，Josef Berg 2摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Lena去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

  Ferdinand Bauer让Lena先查领汤者的洗礼证，没有证的只能领面包。一个男人把证拍在桌上，说自己在战壕里丢的；Lena看了看Ferdinand Bauer，把汤勺放下了。那天剩下的汤被倒进沟里，热气在雪上化出一个小洞。
  _effects: condition; flag +refused_soup_without_certificate; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; knowledge “the parish requires a baptism certificate for soup”_

  Luise Berg 2把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Lena留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Lena在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods); mood -0.010000000000000009; role_

  Friedrich Winter在通行证上盖了章，又用铅笔在角落写了一个日期。Lena拿到许可，可以去城西的修配厂上工；走出劳动局时，Lena把证件摸了三遍，确认它还在口袋里。
  _effects: cash -2 (通行证手续费); role; flag +work_permit_granted; knowledge “城西修配厂只认盖蓝章的通行证。”_

  Lena抱着Ferdinand Kral去医院，走廊里躺满缠绷带的士兵。挂号的人看了一眼孩子的脸，说病床先给伤员。Lena在门口站到天黑，最后用外套把孩子裹紧，走回家去；路上孩子问，医院为什么不收小孩。
  _effects: cash -1 (第三遍换湿布); flag +hospital_refused_child; knowledge “the hospital reserves beds for wounded soldiers”; condition_

- **1942.04** `kernel-social.neighbor-conflict::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/neighbor-conflict.chalk-line` tags: social, neighbor, conflict, everyday, housing, scarcity, vienna, urban
  Katharina Kral把两家叫到楼梯口，用粉笔在地上画了一条线，说煤堆和童车各占一半。邻居先把自己的煤往里挪了半步，Lena也把童车折起来；第二天，线被谁的鞋底蹭花了一半，但煤堆没有再越界。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114; role; flag +painted_floor_line; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

  催款单贴在门框上，写着七天内还清十二克朗。Lena把结婚时的座钟抱去当铺，掌柜只肯给四克朗，还要扣一个月保管费。回家时，Anton Winter 2在楼梯口问钟怎么不见了，Lena说送去修了。
  _effects: cash -8 (催款单贴在门框上); condition; flag +pawned_household_goods; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

  Anton收下了锅，却没有让Lena进门，说东西留下，人先回去。Lena在门口站了一会儿，把锅盖掀开又盖上；第二天，空锅被放在门槛上，里面压着一张写着“收到了”的纸条。
  _effects: role; flag +cold_peace; knowledge “对方只收下东西，没有让进门，也没有提那笔钱。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1943.03** `r13-rel.first-meeting-dance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::first-meeting-dance-postwar/borrowed-shoes` tags: relationship, dance, courtship, acquaintance, leisure, vienna, urban, crisis
  战后的舞会在一间改成礼堂的仓库里举行。Lena的鞋是向邻居借的，大了一指；Anton把自己的鞋带解下来，替Lena把鞋后跟绑紧。两人跳完一支，谁也没提仓库漏雨的屋顶。
  _effects: cash -1 (舞曲换了第三支); role_

- **1943.05** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/shelter` tags: war, homefront, shortage
  警报响时Lena躲进地下室，和十几户人挤在长凳上。头顶传来闷响，灰尘从梁上落下。警报解除后，街上多了一堆碎瓦。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1943.08** `r1-civ.papers-without-heimatschein::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/bought-temporary-paper` tags: migration, papers, police, stateless, work, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena把八克朗夹在登记表里递进窗口。Friedrich Winter数了两遍，把表压在玻璃板下，说“先给你办三个月”。Lena拿到一张手写的临时证明，纸上没有编号，只有一枚模糊的圆章；出门时，Friedrich Winter在背后说“三个月后再来”。
  _effects: cash -8 (没有原籍证明的人); mood 0.030000000000000027; condition; flag +bought_temporary_residence_paper; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000134:child; knowledge “a handwritten temporary paper has no serial number and expir”_

- **1944.01** `r13-civ.midwife::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/safe-delivery` tags: health, midwife, birth, neighbor, night, vienna, urban, crisis
  Luise Stein把热水、剪刀和一卷粗线摆在桌上，让Lena按住Katharina Berg的肩膀。孩子落地时没有哭，Luise Stein倒提着小腿拍了两下，屋里才响起声音。Lena把剪子递过去，手背上全是产妇攥出的红印。
  _effects: cash -3 (夜里敲接生婆的门); flag +attended_safe_delivery; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001119:child_

- **1944.08** `r3-crime.false-accusation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/maid-confesses` tags: crime, court, servant, class, injustice, vienna, urban, crisis
  就在法官要敲槌时，Helene Weiss忽然站起来，说糖罐是她打碎的，碎片埋在院子里的李树下。Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)的脸涨得通红，Ferdinand Stein被叫去后院挖了半个钟头。Lena被当庭释放，门外下着雨，Helene Weiss追出来，把一个纸包塞进Lena手里，里面是三个月的工钱。
  _effects: reputation 0.05000000000000002; flag +cleared_by_maid_confession; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000534:child; knowledge “the sugar bowl was broken and buried in the yard”_

- **1944.09** `r2-leisure.cafe-table::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university::cafe-table-crisis/paid-to-keep-the-table` tags: cafe, regular, routine, class, conversation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Die Fackel在门口贴了“最低消费一克朗”，靠窗的桌子第二天就空了一半。Lena照常点一杯咖啡，坐到中午，Helene Weiss来收杯子时低声说，老板在算您的煤火钱。Lena把报纸折好，留下一枚硬币，说明天还来。
  _effects: cash -2 (靠窗第三张桌子); flag +paid_to_keep_cafe_table; role; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000534:child; knowledge “the owner counts the coal burned by a single customer”_

- **1944.11** `r3-mig.colonial-return::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/trunk-and-quinine` tags: migration, colonial, return, land, family, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil Bauer 2从殖民地回来，皮箱上贴着轮船公司的标签，里面是发霉的衬衫、一包奎宁和几张没有盖章的地契。Lena把床让出来，Emil Bauer 2说那边热得连影子都短。夜里他咳嗽，Lena起来给他倒水。
  _effects: cash -3 (从殖民地回来的皮箱); food; condition; flag +took_in_returning_colonist; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; knowledge “the returnee has land deeds with no stamps”_

- **1944.12** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Emil Winter 2，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Emil Winter 2点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/postwar-illness` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  Lena在战后第二个月病倒，医院没有足够的药，医生只给了退烧粉。邻居每天送来一碗汤，放在门口就走。
  _effects: cash -4 (medicine); condition_

- **1945.01** `r13-work.price-goods::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::crisis-repricing/repriced-at-noon` tags: commerce, pricing, market, competition, shop, vienna, urban, crisis
  早上进的糖，中午就涨了价。Lena把价签擦了又写，写到第三遍时，粉笔只剩一个头。Friedrich Bauer排队时还在数零钱，轮到柜前才发现又贵了两赫勒；Lena按早上的价卖给她，账本上那一行用红笔圈了起来。
  _effects: cash 4 (粉笔写的价签); flag +work13_repriced_goods_in_crisis; knowledge “wholesale prices move faster than the chalkboard”_

- **1945.07** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  Lena在家把一锅扁豆汤分出一半，让Emil端回去给生病的老母亲。Emil第二天还回一只空锅，锅底放着一小块猪油。
  _effects: food; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1945.09** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/quiet-solidarity` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Lena没有发言，只把带来的面包分成几份。散会时，一个不认识的女人把她的地址塞给Lena，说那条街还需要有人继续收会费。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129_

  门铃是根铁丝拉的，响得发闷。Otto Winter 2提篮进门先看水槽里泡着的碗，再看墙角的煤箱，说价钱她心里有数。Lena把印泥盒推到桌边，按下去时指尖凉了一下。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; flag +parish_registered_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 55 -> 45; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 8 -> 15; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 40 -> 58; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 6 -> 2; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled false -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004100:child; flag +wrong_school_register_

  Lena站在书架前抽出一本厚小说，就着门口的光读了半章，Ferdinand Lechner在柜台后抬起眼皮看了一眼，没说话。读到要紧处，Lena摸出铅笔头把页码记在车票背面，把书插回原处，说下次带够押金再来；白读半章，管理员的眼神却凉了一点。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  城门口的旗换了颜色，岗哨开始检查每一张纸。Lena回家时发现街角新钉了一块告示牌：天黑以后不许出门，地窖里的粮食要登记。晚上，巡逻队的靴声从窗下经过，比钟声还准。
  _effects: state_add occupied-city-regime state:world-vienna-1912:203; mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +saw_the_occupation_flag_

  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:204; cash 2 (首演酬劳); role; reputation 0.03; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

  Lena拿着广告上的地址找到那间作坊，看见工人把石灰粉装进印着“法国进口”的纸盒。Anton Huber 3愿意出具成分鉴定，条件是匿名。文章登出后，广告商起诉报社，Emil Huber把Lena的工资扣了半个月。
  _effects: cash -3 (整版广告上的白粉); reputation 0.10000000000000003; flag +exposed_quack_advertisement; knowledge “the medicine is repackaged lime powder”_

  _effects: cash -4 (被征用的粮食折价); food; knowledge “征用收据只在下一批检查前有效。”_

  新排的戏没有坐满。Lena在第二幕听见后排有人笑，不是笑台词，是笑那顶歪掉的王冠。散场后，经理说下一轮要换一个更年轻的国王。
  _effects: state_parameter stage-sovereign.publicBelief 20 -> 12; state_parameter stage-sovereign.scandal 0 -> 6; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Lena把借据推回去，说自己家里也等着米下锅。Berta Berg的脸沉下来，说认识这么多年，连个名字都不肯签。Lena没有解释，只把桌上的茶钱付了；Berta Berg站起来时把椅子带倒，也没有扶。
  _effects: cash -1 (借据上的第二个名字); flag +refused_to_vouch; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000839:child_

- **1947.02** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

  营地要关闭，Katharina Weiss给每人三个选择：回国、留下、去第三国。Lena在表格上写了村名，又划掉，改成“留下”；Luise Bauer 2说学校可以继续办，Emil Weiss 3说留下也没有房子。那天晚上，Lena把三个选择抄在纸上，折成一只船。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +chose_to_stay_after_camp_closure; role; knowledge “the camp is closing and residents must choose”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108_

- **1947.08** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena数出八赫勒交给Anna Bauer，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Anna Bauer说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Lena盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; flag +funeral_society_member_

  Katharina Weiss在通行证上盖了章，又用铅笔在角落写了一个日期。Lena拿到许可，可以去城西的修配厂上工；走出劳动局时，Lena把证件摸了三遍，确认它还在口袋里。
  _effects: cash -2 (通行证手续费); knowledge “城西修配厂只认盖蓝章的通行证。”_

- **1948.01** `state_expire:stage-sovereign` tags: stage-sovereign, performance, theatre, pretend, status, irony, power, no-real-authority

  _effects: state_remove stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:204; flag -stage_sovereign_

  话说到一半，Luise Huber把那只钟摔在地上，玻璃碎了一地。Lena的手背被飞片划出血，Luise Huber还要去抓她的胳膊，被隔壁的男人拦住。当天夜里，Lena把铺盖搬到厨房，门从里面用椅子顶住。
  _effects: cash -2 (把铺盖搬到走廊尽头); condition; flag +violent_separation; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002199:child; relation_end partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002199:child_

  Lena用鞋蜡把裂缝填上，又拿粗针和麻线把鞋底缝了一圈。针脚歪，鞋底一高一低，走路时右脚落地总比左脚重。第二天上工回来，脚后跟磨出一个水泡，可鞋没再渗水。
  _effects: skill_

  锅底刮出焦味，Katharina Weiss说今天就这些了，明天赶早。Lena把空罐子夹在腋下往回走，石板路上结了薄冰，Franz Huber在拐角等着，二话没说把罐子递过来。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000441:child_

- **1949.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family

  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); condition; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001119:child_

  Lena把画挂在画廊最里面的墙角，标签上只写了“无题”。三天里，来看的人从画前走过去，停在旁边的风景画前。闭展时，Lena把画取下来，画框背面落了一层灰。
  _effects: flag +painting_ignored_at_exhibition_

  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Lena看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: health -0.03999999999999998 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  Lena没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011499_

  Lena把最后一袋黑麦埋进煤堆下面，夜里听见巡逻队从窗前走过。第二天面包房照常开门，但货架上少了三排面包；Lena知道那不是因为城里没有粮。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +hidden_grain_

  Lena端着一锅炖肉去敲Hedwig的门，说肉炖多了，一个人吃不完。Hedwig让他进屋，把两只盘子摆在桌上；两人先谈煤价，再谈母亲的坟，最后才提那笔说不清的钱。Alois Bauer 2在楼下等消息，等到灯灭才走。

  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

  Lena告诉Grete Bauer，Anton Bauer家的地窖里还有两桶猪油。征用队下午就去了那户人家。Lena拿到一小袋盐和一张优先配给卡，回家时没有走平时那条街。
  _effects: cash 1 (检举邻居得到的配给奖励); reputation 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001460:child; flag +denounced_neighbor_for_grain_

  Lena站在汤锅后面，把勺子往碗沿上磕两下，再递给下一只手。队伍从门口拐到巷子里，有人端着碗蹲在墙根吃。收摊时，Friedrich Moser把锅底最后一点汤刮给Lena，说今天够了。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +volunteered_at_soup_kitchen; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-153; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-153_

  Anton拒绝加班的话刚落地，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne就把一摞发黄的旧书页推到案头，说这些要按页码理好，星期六之前必须完。Anton没敢再开口，一页页翻到天黑。回家时手指上全是纸毛，工钱袋却和平时一样轻。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; health -0.01999999999999999 (分拣旧书页又多站了一个钟头); relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

- **1928.03** `vienna-apprentice-printshop.union-leaflet/union-leaflet.caught` tags: work, apprentice, factory, social
  Anton把传单卷进裤腰，在门口被Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne叫住。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne抽出纸卷念了两行，说“印刷所不是煽动所”，把学徒补贴减半，并要Friedrich Huber在排字房当众道歉。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (学徒补贴被减半); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1928.06** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-one-visit-only` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Anton去了一个下午，看那些旧歌本里夹着褪色的圣像卡，工厂来的几个年轻人在最后一排用手指敲长凳打拍子。第二个星期天Anton没再去，在院子里帮母亲晾被单。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.010000000000000009; skill_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.leave-bad-workshop/half-measures-stay` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  “换清洗剂要加钱。”Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说完，只让人拆了半扇木板。Anton站在窗口试了试风，知道这已经是能争到的全部。此后每天上午还好，过了中午，太阳穴就开始一跳一跳地疼；Anton把手指在围裙上搓热，继续排字，工钱一分没少。
  _effects: health 0.020000000000000018 (窗户拆掉一半后气味稍减); condition; relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter; cash 1 (保住工位，照常领日薪); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1930.01** `major-task.trade-qualification/journeyman-passed` tags: task, task:work, work, trade, exam, apprenticeship, employment
  考场在印刷厂楼上的装配间。Berta Berg 2把一块松动的版锁推到Anton面前，要求不弄坏铅字重新固定；又让Anton读出自己排的一页价目表。Anton的手一直抖，最后还是把版锁压平了。傍晚，Berta Berg 2在证书上写下“Geselle”，工钱按熟练工重新算。
  _effects: employment Facharbeiter @ org-winter-print; role; cash -4 (examination fee（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; knowledge “版锁要压到铅字不动，又不能把字脚压坏。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1930.10** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anton在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Anton去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral_

- **1931.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Anton把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Anna Bauer没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Anton回头看了一眼，Anna Bauer正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1931.01** `major-crisis.press-accident/hand-crushed` tags: crisis, work, accident, disability, death, printshop, health, injury
  送纸的滚筒没有停稳，Anton的袖口被卷进去，右手跟着进了两个铁辊之间。Lena扑过去扳离合器，机器又转了一圈才停。医院截掉了食指和中指的第二关节；厂里先垫二十四克朗，六周后事故保险结算，工厂说没有适合一只手排字的位置。
  _effects: health -0.18 (right hand crushed between the feed roller and the press); condition; unemployment (the printshop had no composing work for a one-handed worker); cash 24 (accident insurance payment assessed after six weeks); role; flag +industrial_disability; relation colleague with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement_

- **1931.01** `r13-civ.rehearse-play::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rehearsal-broken-up` tags: culture, theatre, rehearsal, play, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  排练到第二幕，房东下来敲门，说楼下的住户嫌吵，再排就涨租。Anton Winter 2把布景折起来，演员们抱着道具站在楼梯口。Anton最后走，关灯时看见舞台地板上有一圈粉笔画的走位线。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +rehearsal_broken_up_by_landlord; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1931.02** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.closed` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard, injury
  Anton把窗关上，争吵声立刻变成闷响，像隔着一层棉被。可那男人的一句话还是从缝里钻进来，她躺到床上翻来覆去，数着墙上的水渍，到很晚才睡着。
  _effects: health -0.02999999999999997 (被楼下争吵搅得没睡好); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1931.07** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.fasching-dance/dance-first-polka` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  波尔卡重新响起时，Anton走到靠墙的长凳前向Anna Bauer弯腰。姑母手里的扇子停了停，没说话。入场费和小费一共一克朗，Anton把找零塞回内袋；一支舞跳完，Anna Bauer说了自己住在哪条街，Anton把名字和门牌在心里念了两遍。
  _effects: cash -1 (付给乐队的小费与舞会入场费); relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Anton在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Anton把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.04999999999999999 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003353_

- **1932.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Anton把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Anton读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Mitzi的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1932.01** `r13-civ.exhibit-painting::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/painting-ignored` tags: culture, painting, exhibition, art, critic, university, schooling, student
  Anton把画挂在画廊最里面的墙角，标签上只写了“无题”。三天里，来看的人从画前走过去，停在旁边的风景画前。闭展时，Anton把画取下来，画框背面落了一层灰。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +painting_ignored_at_exhibition_

- **1932.05** `r3-mig.residency-registration::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-new-register/paid-for-doorplate` tags: migration, registration, housing, papers, bureaucracy, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton交了五克朗登记费和一克朗“门牌费”，Hermann Bauer从抽屉里取出一块蓝底白字的门牌，说钉在门口，警察看见就知道这户登记过。Helene Winter接过门牌，翻过来看了看背面，说钉子要自己买。
  _effects: cash -6 (门牌号与登记簿); condition; flag +paid_registration_and_doorplate_fee; relation colleague with person-helene-winter; knowledge “the blue doorplate signals a registered address”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1932.12** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.belt-patch` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Anton拆下一条旧皮带的头，蒙在鞋底破口上，用钉子一点点敲进去。第三下时锥子扎透鞋面，扎进左手拇指根，血滴在鞋帮上。她用围裙擦了擦，还是把皮子钉完了，第二天走路时鞋底有块硬响。
  _effects: health -0.04999999999999999 (锥子扎伤拇指根); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1933.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household
  Mitzi在几天后找到Anton，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。
  _effects: relation partner with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; household; role; role; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003859_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Anton在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Anton的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1933.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.introduce-two-people/overpraise` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  介绍时把两边都夸过了头，说老友是印厂里最靠得住的人，说女工的手艺整条街都找不出第二个。话太多，两人反倒拘谨起来，各自看窗外。咖啡凉了，坐了不到一个钟头就散了，出门时谁也没提再见面。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和点心); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000134:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1933.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Karl Kraus说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Karl Kraus陪着Anton去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-karl-kraus; mood -0.09999999999999987_

- **1933.09** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.crooked-sleeves` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Anton的剪刀钝得咬布，一剪下去袖口走了斜线。她索性把两只袖子都裁短，袖边用灰线锁了一圈。锁边时线太短，接到第三段才够，右手食指被针顶出个红印，改完像件旧式的短褂。
  _effects: skill_

- **1933.10** `vienna-life-depression/leaves-vienna` tags: adulthood, depression, work, migration, unemployment
  Anton听说下奥地利的砖厂还招人，收拾了一只箱子，坐早班火车离开维也纳。厂里的工头在背后说，走了就别回来。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; unemployment (left the Vienna factory); cash -8 (train ticket and lodgings)_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Anton被叫到学校，老师说孩子这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Anton没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 15; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中0.9克朗记入债务）)_

- **1934.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/house-call-syrup-only` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, rural, agriculture, small_town
  医生没有来，只让药房送来一瓶止咳糖浆。Anton咳了三个星期，左肺留下杂音；Friedrich Huber把窗户钉开一条缝，说新鲜空气比药便宜。后来他连上二楼都要停下来喘，空瓶在窗台上排成一排。瓶口结了一圈灰。
  _effects: cash -4 (cough syrup delivered instead of a visit（其中4克朗记入债务）); health -0.08000000000000002 (three weeks of untreated cough); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; knowledge “请不到医生时，药房会送一瓶止咳糖浆；新鲜空气不要钱，肺里的杂音也不会自己消失。”_

  Anton把窗关上，争吵声立刻变成闷响，像隔着一层棉被。可那男人的一句话还是从缝里钻进来，她躺到床上翻来覆去，数着墙上的水渍，到很晚才睡着。

  Anton在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Anton把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。

  Anton和Mitzi在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Mitzi把煤钱推过来，Anton接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Anton被叫到学校，老师说孩子这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Anton没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。

  Anton说家里没有空地方，把门关到只留一条缝。Anton Lechner的纸箱在楼梯口放了一夜，第二天早上不见了，只剩一圈湿印子。Anton下楼时绕开那块地方走。
  _effects: flag +refused_refugee_shelter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1935.11** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-negotiate-installments` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你没去借钱，直接下楼找房东，说月底前先付两克朗，剩下两克朗分两个星期补齐。房东叼着雪茄，拿铅笔在你的纸条背面写了个日期，说再拖一天就换锁。邻居在楼道口听见了，冲你摇了摇头，但没说什么。
  _effects: cash -2 (按分期付掉的第一笔欠租（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; flag +rent_installment_plan_

- **1935.11** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Helene Winter敲了门。Anton掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Helene Winter什么也没说就走了。夜里Anton听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter_

- **1935.12** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Anton把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Mitzi站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Anton手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; reputation 0.03_

- **1936.01** `statistical_infection`
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1936.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Anton把孩子的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。孩子今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Anton听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 10 -> 18; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 55 -> 52; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Gustav Berg把Anton的工龄册翻到最后一页，又对照了厂里寄来的解雇单，说可以领十二周。钱不多，每周够买面包和煤。Anton在表格上按了手印，Gustav Berg把一张薄薄的领取券推过来，说下周同一时间来，别迟到。
  _effects: cash 6 (补助金窗口的第二张表); flag +work13_unemployment_benefit_approved; knowledge “the benefit runs for twelve weeks and must be collected in p”_

- **1936.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Anton的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1936.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  诊室在二楼，楼梯间飘着石炭酸味。医生把钳子举到灯下看了看，叫Anton攥紧扶手。Emil Huber站在门边数墙上的价目表：拔牙两克朗五十赫勒，药棉五十赫勒，连消炎水另算。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用（其中3克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1936.05** `statistical_recovery`
  Anton从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1936.08** `r1-fam.gossip-campaign::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/gossip.unresolved` tags: social, gossip, reputation, market, rumour, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton去找Luise Kral 3对质，Luise Kral 3说不是她说的，是洗衣房的人说的。两人一路问到洗衣房，洗衣房的人说是市场卖鱼的说的；最后谁也没找到源头。Anton回到家，发现门口被人用粉笔画了一个圈，孩子在圈里跳房子。
  _effects: reputation -0.12; condition; flag +rumour_without_a_source; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  Josef Winter说今年的摊位排满了，收了Anton两克朗的巷口管理费，才往墙外指了指。Anton把秤放上独轮车，Otto Bauer 2路过，问要不要把自家门廊借给他摆一上午，条件是替她看两小时孩子。秤盘在风里晃，绳子磨得发白，Anton看了看天，把车把转向巷口。
  _effects: cash -2 (棚下的第三个摊位（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +denied_market_stall; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 18 -> 26; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 52 -> 49; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; flag +parish_registered; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

  Anton在诊所门口从五点半站到十一点，膝盖发僵，终于用最后四十赫勒付了挂号费。门房撕下转诊单，叫Anton去综合医院排队，药费还得另算；队伍拐过街角时天还黑着，Anton数着前面二十三个后脑勺，记住了综合医院药房下午才开窗口。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (诊所挂号费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); knowledge “综合医院的药房比街角药房便宜，但要排到下午”_

  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -20 (escape and lodging（其中19克朗记入债务）); condition_

  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; role; flag +cold_peace; knowledge “对方只收下东西，没有让进门，也没有提那笔钱。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1938.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1938.09** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Anton把Alois Berg和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Anton把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1938.10** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household
  Anton把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Anton的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart（其中8克朗记入债务）)_

  Anton把最后一袋黑麦埋进煤堆下面，夜里听见巡逻队从窗前走过。第二天面包房照常开门，但货架上少了三排面包；Anton知道那不是因为城里没有粮。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +hidden_grain; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

  上诉期的最后一天，Anton把申诉书交到区公所。Franz Bauer看了一眼钟，说邮戳是昨天的，算数。驱逐令暂缓，等上面复核。Emil Bauer 3在门口等着，听见“暂缓”两个字，把已经掏出来的手铐又放回口袋。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +appeal_filed_on_last_day; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; knowledge “a postmark from the day before keeps the appeal alive”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

  他在站台坐了一夜，第二天买了一张回程票。Gustav Winter来退押金时扣下两克朗，说床板被他压裂了；他没有争，提着箱子走回车站，觉得城里的钟走得比乡下快。回到乡下后，他把城里的车票贴在账本第一页。
  _effects: move place-wien; cash -1 (回程票和押金扣款（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +returned_to_station; knowledge “进城找工要先有落脚处，否则押金和回程票会把路费吃光”; health -0.010000000000000002 (在站台坐了一夜)_

- **1939.05** `vienna-illness-household.wage-earner-injury/report-unsafe` tags: health, family, home
  Anton和工友围住Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne，指着冲床说这机器要修。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne骂完还是叫人来钉护板；Luise Bauer 2的手没全好，厂里却多了块木板，Anton也多了个“爱出头”的名声。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; condition; reputation 0.1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108_

- **1939.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Anton胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Anton没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006870_

  _effects: birth male; cash -18 (doctor and medicine（其中18克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:158_

  _effects: cash -4 (被征用的粮食折价（其中4克朗记入债务）); flag +requisition_receipt; knowledge “征用收据只在下一批检查前有效。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

  钱字到了嘴边，Anton改了口，问Anna Berg 2铺子里要不要一个周末帮工，搬货、上架、跑腿都行。Anna Berg 2上下打量他一番，说周六早上六点来，一天一克朗，干不好就不留。Anton应下，回家路上算了算，要干满五个周末才够房租。
  _effects: cash 1 (周末帮工的工钱); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; flag +weekend_shop_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

  物价一天涨三次，Maria Stein把面粉袋上的日期改小，Anton在店里看见他拿钢笔描数字。Emil Winter 2来买面包，Maria Stein说“今天只剩黑的”；Anton把自己刚买的一袋白面放在柜台上，说“那这个呢”。
  _effects: flag +exposed_merchant_date_fraud; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; knowledge “the merchant alters flour dates”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

  第一面旗挂反了，蓝边朝上，Elisabeth Bauer在梯子上骂了一句，Anton在下面扶着梯子笑。街角还有零星的枪声，Franz Lechner把藏了三年的葡萄酒搬出来，瓶塞一开，泡沫喷了Anton一脸。Emil Bauer 3带着人从教堂那边过来，说“先别庆祝，仓库还在烧”。
  _effects: mood 0.16000000000000003; flag +raised_flag_on_liberation_day; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; relation friend with person-franz-lechner; knowledge “the depot is still burning”_

  Friedrich Winter说名单上有一个同姓的人，住在城北的收容站。Luise Kral 3连夜赶去，见到的却是一个比她儿子年轻的裁缝。Anton陪她走回来，路上Luise Kral 3一直说名字没有错，是年份错了；Anton没有纠正，只把她的胳膊挽得更紧。
  _effects: cash -1 (念错的名字（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +followed_a_false_lead; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; knowledge “the name on the list belongs to another family”_

  城门口的旗换了颜色，岗哨开始检查每一张纸。Anton回家时发现街角新钉了一块告示牌：天黑以后不许出门，地窖里的粮食要登记。晚上，巡逻队的靴声从窗下经过，比钟声还准。
  _effects: state_add occupied-city-regime state:world-vienna-1912:168; mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +saw_the_occupation_flag_

- **1941.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money
  下午的活是搬纱锭，Anton蹲下去再站起来眼前发黑，扶墙站了一会儿。工友老约瑟夫把自己的水杯推过来，说去诊所开个假条吧，她摇头，说假条要扣钱。
  _effects: condition; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

- **1941.12** `kernel-social.neighbor-conflict::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/neighbor-conflict.coal-heap` tags: social, neighbor, conflict, everyday, housing, scarcity, vienna, urban
  邻居把煤堆在走廊拐角，Anton的童车推不过去，只能把煤往墙边扫。邻居听见声响，出来说煤是自己的，谁动谁赔；Anton把扫帚靠在墙上，两人站在煤灰里，谁也不肯先回屋。Franz Bauer在楼下听见，拿粉笔在地上画了一道线。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000839:child; role; cash -1 (a new broom for the coal dust（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +corridor_feud; knowledge “门房在走廊地上画了线，煤堆不得越过。”_

  Friedrich Winter在通行证上盖了章，又用铅笔在角落写了一个日期。Anton拿到许可，可以去城西的修配厂上工；走出劳动局时，Anton把证件摸了三遍，确认它还在口袋里。
  _effects: cash -2 (通行证手续费（其中2克朗记入债务）); role; flag +work_permit_granted; knowledge “城西修配厂只认盖蓝章的通行证。”_
