  Anton等到月底才想起保险册还在抽屉里，印花已经过期。Friedrich Winter说下个月可以补，但要重新体检。Anton把册子翻到空白页，看见前面一栏写着“自愿放弃”。
  _effects: flag +insurance_enrolment_lapsed; knowledge “a lapsed stamp can be renewed only after a new medical exami”_

- **1942.02** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Hedwig手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Hedwig把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Anton记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; flag +seeking_work_

- **1942.03** `r13-rel.friendship-after-separation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/civil-at-the-corner` tags: relationship, separation, friendship, ex_partner, civility, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton在街角遇见Luise Kral，两人同时停了一下。Luise Kral问Anton的母亲身体怎么样，Anton说还好，又问Luise Kral的新住处漏不漏雨。Emil Kral在旁边的摊子付钱，装作没听见。临走时，两人互相点了下头。
  _effects: role; relation friend with person-luise-kral; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1942.03** `r15-work.factory-hiring::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hired` tags: work, factory, employment, industrial, wage, vienna, urban, crisis
  受监管的工人合作社把Anton的名字写在班次表上。工钱不高，工时很长，但下个星期开始有固定的收入。Anton回家时绕路买了一小块肥皂。
  _effects: employment factory worker @ org:world-vienna-1912:受监管的工人合作社:164; state_add factory-employment state:world-vienna-1912:174_

- **1943.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/leave-vienna` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership, migration
  两张车票分开买，Anton在月台这头，Emil Hofer 2在那头。火车开动后两人才在车厢连接处站到一起，谁也没有说话，只把行李往同一张座位底下推。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; cash -12 (train tickets and lodging（其中12克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:factory-employment/shift-year/hurt` tags: factory-employment, work, factory, employment, industrial, state, state_tick, injury
  Anton在换班时被机器擦伤了手臂。工头让她去水龙头下面冲一冲，然后回来继续。
  _effects: health -0.05 (factory accident); condition; state_parameter factory-employment.injured false -> true_

- **1943.01** `p10-migration-tasks.ask-travel-money/uncle-refuse-introduce-carrier` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, loan, relative
  舅父听完摇了摇头，说钱不借，倒可以给他写张条子，介绍一个跑布吕恩的车夫，让他搭便车走。舅母把点心收进橱里，说路上总得带点吃的。他收了条子，坐车夫的车走了两天。
  _effects: relation kin with person-luise-kral; cash -1 (路上给车夫买烟叶的道谢钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1943.04** `vienna-social-movement.aftermath/burn-the-list` tags: development, social_movement, organization, consequence
  Anton在厨房的炉子里烧掉名单。纸灰卷起来，像黑色的叶子。Anton仍然去上工，但从那以后不再在公开场合提起Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung。
  _effects: organization_membership; role_

- **1943.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Anton就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Anton Winter摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状)_

- **1943.11** `p9-work-printshop.type-case/borrow-box` tags: work, printshop, printing, compositor, craft, workshop, typesetting, rush_order
  Anton把围裙一解，穿过街去对面印厂借一盒同号小写铅字，押了半克朗。回来时Luise Kral已把广告标题排好，两人赶在午饭前上机印完五百份，油墨蹭了满手。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (押给对面印厂的铅字保钱（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); skill; relation colleague with person-luise-kral_

- **1944.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Anton在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Anton把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008709_

  Anton和Emil Lechner一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Anton去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Anton先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); health -0.019999999999999997 (在病人屋里待久了，嗓子发干); flag +parish_sick_visitor_

  Anton刚提一句“那年分东西”，Lena就把勺子放下，说柜子是他先看中的，凭什么让。话越说越硬，汤还剩半碗，Lena起身拿了帽子，椅子在石地上刮出一声。Anton一个人坐到教堂的钟敲完，把两份饭钱都付了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; cash -0.4 (付了两份饭钱)_

  _effects: cash -14 (先垫付母亲全部药费（其中14克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +claims_mother_room_

  _effects: cash -3 (延迟偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; reputation -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

  _effects: role; reputation 0.020000000000000004_

  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

  _effects: unemployment (厂里裁员结清工钱); cash 14 (结清的工钱和补偿)_

  配给站少了一袋面粉，Anna Winter说看见Anton最后一个离开。Anton没有争，只把自己的配给证摊在桌上，上面还粘着当天的印泥。Anna Winter看了很久，最后说也许是自己记错了。

  报亭后面的墙根下，Hedwig把咬了一半的糖递过去，换回一张火车头画片。糖纸被风吹到摊主的脚边。Franz Berg弯腰捡起来，把画片从Hedwig手里抽走，说先把地扫了。Hedwig扫到墙根，Franz Berg才把画片还回来。
  _effects: mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1921.12** `r1-child.bully-teacher-son::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/fought-in-the-snow` tags: bullying, school, teacher, class, fear, university, culture, schooling
  Hedwig在放学路上把教师儿子推倒在雪里，两个人滚成一团。Maria Weiss赶来时，先扶起自己的儿子，再问Hedwig为什么打人。Hedwig说她先切我的橡皮。Maria Weiss说一块橡皮也值得动手？那天Hedwig在走廊站到天黑。
  _effects: reputation -0.08; flag +fought_teachers_son; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-refused-nuggets` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  Hedwig空手站在窗口，听掌柜说“叫你Josefine Hofer来”，耳朵一直红到脖子。后墙的渣堆冻得硬，Hedwig用鞋尖刨开表层，把煤核一颗颗捡进围裙。回家路上碰到同巷的孩子，Hedwig把纸袋往身后藏，进门才交给Josefine Hofer。
  _effects: mood -0.04; food; skill_

- **1922.03** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  土豆从围裙里滚出来，Hedwig蹲下去追，手心一松，三枚十克鲁泽的铜板叮叮当当掉在石板路上。两枚捡回来了，最后一枚滚进排水格栅，在铁条下转了个圈。Hedwig趴下去够，指甲刮在铁条上。回家Josefine Hofer数完钱，脸色沉下来，Hedwig站在桌边不敢动。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道（其中0.3克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; mood -0.06_

- **1922.05** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.win` tags: school, social, childhood
  拼写比赛结束时，Franz Stein敲了敲讲台说“记住这个词”。Hedwig袖口上多了一枚小十字章，回家路上Emil追着要看，最后用三颗李子换了眼福。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; mood 0.1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; food_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Hedwig站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Gustav Berg在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Hedwig掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Hedwig才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1924.01** `r13-edu.ask-teacher::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/teacher-explains` tags: education, teacher, help, school, confidence, university, culture, schooling
  Hedwig在办公室门口站到下课，才敢把作业本递进去。Anna Bauer翻到错得最多的那一页，没有骂，只让Hedwig把题目再读一遍。读到第三遍，Hedwig忽然明白自己一直看漏了条件。Anna Bauer把红笔放下，说以后不懂就来。那天以后，Hedwig的作业本上多了几行红字，都是Anna Bauer夜里改的。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-anna-bauer_

- **1924.08** `r3-crime.juvenile-court::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/admonished-and-sent-home` tags: crime, youth, court, family, school, university, culture, schooling
  Josef Winter把案卷合上，问Hedwig知不知道错了。Hedwig的腿在长椅上晃着，够不着地，说“知道”。Josefine Hofer在旁边一直点头，Alois Stein把一张写着“训诫”的纸递给她。出门时，Josefine Hofer把纸折好放进内衣口袋，怕人看见。
  _effects: flag +admonished_by_juvenile_court; role; relation guardian with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the juvenile court keeps a record even without a sentence”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1925.01** `major-education-scholarship-examination/full-scholarship` tags: education, scholarship, adolescence, major
  考试设在女子中学的二层教室。Grete Stein当着Hedwig的面拆开牛皮纸袋：一篇作文、两道算术、一段听写。Hedwig写到最后一个数字时，手心里全是墨。三周后学校寄来通知，学费全免，首年发三十克朗书籍费，余下按学期发；条件是期末平均分不得低于良。
  _effects: education secondary; cash 30 (annual scholarship grant); role; flag +lyceum_scholarship; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1925.01** `kernel-edu.prepare-for-exam::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-prepare-success` tags: task, adolescence, school, exam, study, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Hedwig从Lena那里借来一本旧课本，缺了最后十页。两人每晚在厨房桌上抄题，Lena念，Hedwig写。考试那天，最后一道大题正好是抄过的题型，Hedwig把答案写得满满当当。成绩出来那天，Hedwig把借来的课本还了回去。
  _effects: skill; skill; reputation 0.039999999999999994; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; flag +prepared_with_borrowed_book_

- **1926.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/printshop-apprentice` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Hedwig下周一到排字房报到，先搬铅字和扫地，月薪十八克朗。家里用第一笔预付工资买了双结实的鞋。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance); role_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.save-for-books/money-taken-for-coal` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Josefine Hofer把火柴盒放回窗台，说等发了工钱再补给他。Hedwig把抄好的纸袋叠成小册子，用缝衣针别在一起，封面写了书名，字比平时大。
  _effects: cash -2 (积蓄被挪去买煤（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06; skill; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1926.04** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/borrow-notes-at-home` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  学费凑不出来，Hedwig就向同屋的学徒借笔记。晚上把灶台边的桌子擦干净，用铅笔头把讲义抄在旧账单背面，Otto Bauer 2允他每两周交一次抄本抵作练习。抄到第三页时灯芯烧短了，字迹开始发颤。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; skill; mood 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.negotiate-hours/refused-overtime` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  Hedwig拒绝加班的话刚落地，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne就把一摞发黄的旧书页推到案头，说这些要按页码理好，星期六之前必须完。Hedwig没敢再开口，一页页翻到天黑。回家时手指上全是纸毛，工钱袋却和平时一样轻。
  _effects: mood -0.05; health -0.020000000000000004 (分拣旧书页又多站了一个钟头); relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1927.07** `vienna-apprentice-printshop.lead-type-drop/lead-type-drop.repaired` tags: work, apprentice, factory, social, injury
  Alois Berg把散落的铅字按字母补回字盘，Hedwig赶在午休前重新排完价目表。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用指甲划过一行，说“排得还直”，没扣钱，只让把过道扫干净。
  _effects: skill; health -0.009999999999999995 (蹲着捡铅字与扫过道); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/joins-union` tags: adolescence, labour, union
  工会的人在厂门口拦住Hedwig，说学徒工也该有工票和保险。Hedwig每月交一克朗会费，把会员证塞进上衣内袋。
  _effects: role; cash -1 (union dues（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1928.01** `task-vienna-poor-apprentice.request-advance/borrow-from-workmates` tags: adolescence, apprentice, work, education, poverty, printshop, task, task:work
  他没再向师傅开口，转身找两个年纪大些的工友各借了两克朗，答应发薪当天就还。人家让他写在烟盒背面按了指印，他把烟盒折好塞进帽檐。
  _effects: cash 4 (向工友借钱应急); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.ask-a-mentor/asked-to-come-back-later` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Josef Weiss看了看Hedwig抄的字母表，说大小不一，让他回去用直尺画格子重抄一遍，下个月再来。少年把纸折好，在咖啡馆门口站了一会儿，走回印刷厂时绕到排字房窗外看了很久。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill; flag +mentor_pending_retry; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

- **1929.04** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/retake-fee` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, rural, agriculture, small_town
  成绩单上差两分。Anna Bauer说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Josefine Hofer来签字。Josefine Hofer在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Hedwig把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.15000000000000002; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1929.12** `vienna-apprentice-printshop.overtime-wage-scuffle/overtime-wage-scuffle.paid-short` tags: work, apprentice, factory, social, injury
  为赶制市议会选举的传单，Hedwig在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne连排三个晚上。结账时Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把加班算成“学徒练习”，只多给半克朗。Alois Berg在楼梯间低声说这是常事，让Hedwig周末别来，去集市八角亭替人抄货单。
  _effects: cash 0.5 (三晚加班只补半克朗); health -0.04999999999999999 (连续三晚排字熬到十点); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1930.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.04999999999999999 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002868_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Franz Berg的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1930.01** `r13-edu.literacy-class::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signs-own-name` tags: education, literacy, adult_education, class, belonging, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig在石板上写自己的名字，写到第三遍才把最后一笔收住。Grete Stein把Hedwig的手按在粉笔上，说慢慢来。放学时，Hedwig把石板抱在怀里，经过肉铺时第一次看懂招牌上写的是“今日有肝”。那天晚上，Hedwig在账本封皮上又写了一遍，写到最后一笔时手没有抖。
  _effects: mood 0.08000000000000002; flag +learned_to_sign_name; skill_

- **1930.06** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family, injury
  Hedwig和Emil Bauer 2一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Hedwig去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Hedwig先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); health -0.020000000000000018 (在病人屋里待久了，嗓子发干); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; flag +parish_sick_visitor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1930.12** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/wage-question-silence` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  Josefine Hofer问起工钱和房租，Hedwig报了个数，又补了一句冬天活少。桌上一时只剩刀叉声。Otto Winter 2低着头把面包掰成小块，Josefine Hofer往汤里又加了一勺盐，谁都没再开口；这顿饭把两个人的将来问成了一个没答完的数目。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1931.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction
  Franz Berg没有答应，只说现在不合适。Hedwig把这件事收回去，之后见面仍点头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003173_

- **1931.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/denounced` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, reform, medical
  Karl Kraus把Hedwig在夜里说过的话整理成三页纸，交到了办事处。纸上没有一句是假的，但每一句都离开了原来的房间。第二天，Hedwig被叫去问话，问话的人已经知道该问什么。Karl Kraus在走廊里遇见Hedwig，点了头，像只是借过一支笔。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:72; reputation -0.06; relation rival with person-karl-kraus; flag +denounced_by_known_person; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1931.01** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig听见墙里有一声很轻的响。第二天，Karl Kraus病倒了；没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 0 -> 1; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003496_

- **1931.01** `r13-fam.apologize::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/forgiven` tags: task, social, apology, reputation, conflict, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig在院子的水龙头边等到Anton Winter 2，把那天酒桌上的话重复了一遍，没有找补。Anton Winter 2拧上水龙头，说那句话已经传到第三个院子了。Hedwig问要怎样才能收回，Anton Winter 2说收不回，但可以当着传话的人再说一遍是胡说的。
  _effects: reputation 0.04000000000000001; flag +apology_accepted; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; knowledge “the insult has already reached three courtyards”_

- **1931.07** `r1-cul.faith-crisis::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/stopped-communion` tags: faith, doubt, confession, church, grief, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig停止领圣餐，把念珠收进抽屉。Anton Winter 2来过两次，第一次带汤，第二次只坐了一会儿。第三周，Franz Berg邀Hedwig去听一场葬礼上的管风琴；Hedwig站在教堂最后面，没有跪下，却把整首曲子听完了。
  _effects: mood 0.02999999999999997; condition; flag +stopped_taking_communion; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; knowledge “the funeral organist played the whole piece without a break”_

- **1931.08** `major-education-apprenticeship-exam/passes-and-stays` tags: education, apprenticeship, work, exam, major, employment
  出师考试在排字房后面那间小屋举行。Hedwig按Anton Huber 3的口令拣字、装版、锁紧版框，再拉出一张校样；一个字母倒着站，被Anton Huber 3用指甲敲了出来。成绩通过，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne从下月起按日计酬，月钱七十二克朗，工牌上添了“排字工”三个字，柜上预支三克朗。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzer @ org-winter-print; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; cash 3 (first journeyman advance); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Hedwig在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Hedwig的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

  Lena托人带话，说只要Hedwig出钱修好教堂的侧门，证词就可以当作没有写过。Hedwig把钱数了三遍，最后交出去。侧门修好了，Lena在门框上刻了一行小字，刻的是Hedwig的名字。
  _effects: cash -3 (修教堂侧门的封口钱); state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 20 -> 5; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.020000000000000004; flag +paid_to_close_accusation_

- **1932.01** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/hold-list-and-watch` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anton把会费账本推回去，说外头那个组织答应给煤，只要名单。Hedwig把账本合上，用一块旧布包好塞进缝纫机底下，说先看看谁在往外递话。两人都没再提这事。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.02999999999999997; flag +kept_district_list_private_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 25 -> 35; reputation -0.04; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱); mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1932.01** `r13-civ.hide-fugitive::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hid-for-three-nights` tags: politics, fugitive, shelter, risk, solidarity, reform, medical, schooling
  Grete Weiss在阁楼的煤箱后面躲了三天，Hedwig每天夜里送一次面包和水。第三天，楼下有人问起生人味，Hedwig说那是煤烟。Grete Weiss走时，把一枚磨亮的纽扣留在窗台上。
  _effects: cash -1 (阁楼上的脚步声); food; flag +hid_fugitive_three_nights; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000261:child; knowledge “the fugitive crossed the yard before the morning roll call”_

- **1932.08** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Elisabeth Bauer敲了门。Hedwig掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Elisabeth Bauer什么也没说就走了。夜里Hedwig听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003490_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-depression/leaves-vienna` tags: adulthood, depression, work, unemployment
  Hedwig听说下奥地利的砖厂还招人，收拾了一只箱子，坐早班火车离开维也纳。厂里的工头在背后说，走了就别回来。
  _effects: unemployment (left the Vienna factory); cash -8 (train ticket and lodgings)_

- **1933.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/refuses-the-money` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, rural
  Friedrich Huber 2把钱推过来，Hedwig又推回去。Hedwig说自己只是倒霉，不是什么会看事的人。Friedrich Huber 2把钱留在桌上走了，第二天又派人来取。Hedwig把那一夜省下来的蜡烛收进柜子，没有点过。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; flag +refused_curse_work; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/whispers-fade` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  一年过去，Hedwig没有再点蜡烛，也没有再去那家后间。教堂的登记册翻到了新的一页，旧页边缘留着指甲划过的痕迹；街上的人开始讨论别的事。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 5 -> 0; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `p10-edu-work-tasks.qualification-exam/p10-edu-work-tasks.qualification-exam.passed` tags: adulthood, education, work, task, night_school, apprenticeship, career, exam
  速记那场他写得手腕发酸，符号连成一串。考完他和其他考生在走廊里对答案，有人叹气有人笑。三天后他来取证明，学校的门房认得他，说商号那边正缺人。
  _effects: cash -5 (商业学校校外考生的报名费); education secondary; skill; reputation 0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  Hedwig和Otto Winter 2在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Otto Winter 2把煤钱推过来，Hedwig接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; mood -0.02999999999999997_

- **1934.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/sold-the-letter` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, rural, agriculture
  Mitzi把Hedwig托付保管的一封旧信卖给了收旧纸的人。信里提到几个已经不用的名字，买主又把信转给了另一个买主。Hedwig再见到那封信时，它被压在一块玻璃下面，标价是Hedwig半个月的工钱。
  _effects: cash -1 (买回自己的旧信); reputation -0.05; relation rival with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “我写的一封旧信被人压在玻璃下面标价出售。”; flag +private_letter_sold_

- **1934.01** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig自己发起了高烧；Mitzi第二天照常出门。没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (the curse turns back on its holder); mood -0.06_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004643_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 35 -> 45; reputation -0.039999999999999994; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1934.01** `task-vienna-activist.speak-meeting/speak-meeting-yields-floor` tags: politics, activism, union, party, leaflet, meeting, task, task:politics
  他站到桌边，看见旁边老工人的手一直举着，指节上全是裂口。他把发言让了出去，自己坐回长凳上听。老工人讲完，主持人问还有没有补充，他摇了摇头，把口袋里写好的提纲又折了一道。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; flag +yielded_floor_at_meeting; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

- **1934.02** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.default` tags: home, market, food, social
  玛丽亚在市场拱门下拦住Hedwig，声音不大但周围人都听得见：三个克朗。Hedwig翻遍口袋只摸出几个十字币。玛丽亚把布袋从Hedwig胳膊上解下来，转身走了。
  _effects: food; reputation -0.15; mood -0.10000000000000003_

- **1934.08** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Hedwig难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Anna 2；Hedwig一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Otto Winter 2把钟表送进当铺。Hedwig落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.21999999999999997 (difficult childbirth); condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:99_

- **1935.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sells-the-mourning` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Luise Weiss 2请Hedwig去家里坐一夜，说只要Hedwig在灵堂待到天亮，那个欠钱的人就会自己上门。Hedwig收了钱，在灵堂坐到天亮，什么也没有发生。一个月后，那个人真的来了，送来一袋面粉；Luise Weiss 2把面粉分给Hedwig一半，没有解释。
  _effects: cash 3 (替人守灵的酬劳); reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; flag +sold_mourning_service; condition_

- **1935.01** `state_expire:suspected-witchcraft` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural

  _effects: state_remove suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:72_

- **1935.01** `p10-edu-work-tasks.apply-scholarship/p10-edu-work-tasks.apply-scholarship.missing-letter` tags: adulthood, education, work, task, night_school, apprenticeship, career, scholarship
  他缺第二封推荐信——Otto Bauer 2只肯写一封，说另一封该由雇主出。窗口的Hedwig被告知明天下午再来。他第二天请假赶到时，走廊里贴出新告示：本学期名额已满。官员建议他等下一学期，他把材料收回布里，没说话。
  _effects: cash -1 (两次跑窗口的车钱与请假扣的工钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1935.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Anton Winter借给Hedwig一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Hedwig睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; mood -0.05999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

  Hedwig认出当票上的编号，那是Gustav Weiss上月被偷的表。Emil Bauer 2说货是别人送来的，Hedwig没有接表，直接去街口找Josef Winter。回来时，柜台上多了一张查封单，Gustav Weiss在门外哭得说不出话，手里还攥着那块擦表的绒布。
  _effects: flag +reported_stolen_pawn; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; knowledge “the pawnbroker bought a stolen watch”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1935.11** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Hedwig把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Alois Lechner站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Hedwig手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; reputation 0.03_

  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Josef 2。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Otto Winter 2把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Hedwig躺在床上出了一身虚汗。

  Hedwig把两次背叛和两次发烧的日期写在同一张纸上，发现中间都隔着十一天。Hedwig没有告诉任何人，只把纸折成很小的一块，塞进缝衣盒的夹层。那天夜里，Hedwig第一次试着在门口撒盐，动作熟练得让自己停了一下。
  _effects: knowledge “背叛与发烧之间隔着十一天。”; flag +curse_counted; skill; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  档案室比阅览室更早关门。Hedwig刚写明要调阅的登记号，管钥匙的人已经挂上了铁钩。他只能凭记忆把脚注补个大概，用半截铅笔在信封背面写下“待核”两个字，走出图书馆时天已全黑。
  _effects: cash -0.2 (次日再跑一趟所需的电车票); mood -0.040000000000000036; skill_

- **1938.01** `kernel-migrate.refugee-aid::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/civic-refugee-search` tags: migration, refugee, missing_relative, care, postwar, vienna, urban, crisis
  名单墙前挤着十几个人，Gustav Winter指着一行被划掉的名字，说那是她妹妹。她替对方抄下名字，又去Emil Bauer 3的窗口问；窗口说档案在另一栋楼，她去时那栋楼已经锁门。她在档案楼门口等到锁门，只记下了一个门牌号。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; knowledge “名单上被划掉的名字要查另一栋楼的档案，窗口关门后只能等明天”; cash -3 (抄名单的纸笔和跑两栋楼的车钱（其中3克朗记入债务）); flag +searched_refugee_list_

- **1938.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.beer-with-senior` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Hedwig把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1939.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Otto Winter 2在楼梯间坐了很久；Hedwig在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Hedwig的名字；此后邻居提起Hedwig，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）); role_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig被叫到学校，老师说孩子这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Hedwig没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 22 -> 17; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 61 -> 66; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.01** `r13-fam.collect-funeral-money::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/doors-closed` tags: task, social, funeral, neighbor, charity, grief, vienna, urban
  Hedwig敲到第三家时，门后的人说这月已经随过两份份子。再往上走，走廊里只剩炒菜的油烟味；Hedwig在楼梯口站了一会儿，把帽子里的钱整理好，自己又添了一克朗。回到Luise Kral家时，只说大家都难。
  _effects: cash -1 (端着一顶帽子走楼道（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +funeral_donations_refused; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说孩子这周主动擦了黑板。Hedwig把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 2 -> 5; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Franz Hofer在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Hedwig把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1939.04** `r13-turn.refugee-arrival::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::war-refugee-columns/turned-away` tags: migration, refugee, papers, housing, displacement, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil Bauer 3说今天的号发完了，让Hedwig明天再来。Hedwig问能不能在走廊里等到天亮，Emil Bauer 3说这里不许过夜。Hedwig把湿行李拖到街边，坐在门口的石阶上，看见里面的人一队队领到热汤。Emil Bauer 3关窗时，把百叶帘拉了下来。
  _effects: cash -3 (登记处门外的湿行李（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +refused_refugee_registration; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; knowledge “the registration office stops issuing numbers before noon”_

- **1939.06** `r1-civ.black-market-cafe::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/bought-flour-on-black-market` tags: scarcity, black market, cafe, police, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  咖啡馆后间的桌上放着一袋面粉，袋口用蓝线缝着，Anton Huber 2用勺子舀出一勺，让Hedwig看颜色。价钱是官价的三倍，Anton Huber 2说“嫌贵可以去排队”。Hedwig数了钱，Grete Weiss 2在门口望风，听见门铃响就敲两下墙。
  _effects: cash -12 (咖啡馆后间的面粉袋（其中12克朗记入债务）); food; mood 0.020000000000000018; condition; flag +bought_flour_on_black_market; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97; knowledge “the back room is entered through the kitchen and the waiter ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97_

- **1940.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/it-turns-in-the-hand` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Hedwig收了钱，却在灵堂里试着把那个名字指向Emil Winter 2。蜡烛刚点着，Hedwig自己的手先抖起来，火苗顺着袖口爬上去。Emil Winter 2扑灭了火，没有喊人，只把门打开。Hedwig走出去时，听见身后有人在数剩下的蜡烛。
  _effects: reputation -0.07; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.severity 0.6 -> 0.7; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:159; flag +curse_recoiled_on_holder; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

  Hedwig把孩子的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。孩子今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Hedwig听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 11 -> 19; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 81 -> 78; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  舅父从裤袋里数出十五克朗推过来，说剩下的让他去车站帮车夫装货抵工钱。舅父在灯下写了一张欠条，写明三个月内还清，他签了名，把钱对折放进内袋，欠条留给舅父收在账簿里。
  _effects: cash 15 (舅父借给的路费); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; flag +owes_uncle_15_kronen; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94_

- **1940.05** `r3-war.blackout::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/warned-about-curtain-gap` tags: war, blackout, warden, neighbor, air_raid, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig在街口吹哨，说一户人家的窗帘缝里漏光，把整面墙照得像一张白纸。屋里的人把灯罩转到墙边，光还是从补丁的针脚里钻出来；Ferdinand Bauer在对面窗后看，手里也捏着一块黑布。Emil Bauer 3走过来，记下门牌，说下次要罚。
  _effects: cash -2 (窗帘缝里的一线光（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +warned_about_blackout_curtain_gap; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1940.06** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/private-household` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership, household
  Hedwig和Luise Berg 2合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; household; mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1941.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/denounced` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, vienna, urban
  Emil Bauer 2把Hedwig在夜里说过的话整理成三页纸，交到了办事处。纸上没有一句是假的，但每一句都离开了原来的房间。第二天，Hedwig被叫去问话，问话的人已经知道该问什么。Emil Bauer 2在走廊里遇见Hedwig，点了头，像只是借过一支笔。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:170; reputation -0.06; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1941.01** `state_reaction:suspected-witchcraft/another-name` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  Emil Bauer 2在指控Hedwig的人面前添了一句话，说Hedwig曾经在夜里念过谁的名字。那句话没有证据，但登记册上又多了一行。Hedwig后来才知道，添这句话的人就是Emil Bauer 2。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 20 -> 30; reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007561_

- **1941.01** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig自己发起了高烧；Emil Bauer 2第二天照常出门。没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007561_

- **1941.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-teaching` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Hedwig的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Hedwig被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。
  _effects: cash -4 (reduced fee for a teaching-house call); health 0.03 (examined by a teaching team); condition; role; knowledge “教学出诊会有实习医生和学生轮流听诊，最后请上级定方案，药费可以减半。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

- **1941.02** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.labour-exchange/exchange-trial-work` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, labour_exchange, bureaucracy
  洗衣房里蒸汽弥漫，熨斗压在衬衫领子上咝咝作响。老板娘翻了翻Hedwig熨好的第一件，说领口还差些火候，手上倒还稳。两打熨完，三个克朗放在围裙口袋里，铜板还带着体温。
  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1941.03** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.fish-heads` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  鱼摊案板上的冰碴子踩在脚下咯吱响。Friedrich Huber把鱼鳃抠干净了才递过来，说这几个头是今天卖剩的鲤鱼。Hedwig回家烧了半锅水，姜片一下锅，厨房里那股腥气就压下去了一半。
  _effects: food; health 0.019999999999999997 (喝上了热鱼汤); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1941.12** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/stay-and-tidy-chairs` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  椅子叠到一半，Josef Berg 2压低声音说今晚有三个人没投票就走了。Hedwig把最后一块瓜子壳扫进簸箕，说记住那三张脸，比上台多说一句有用。灯熄时大厅只剩木头发出的轻响。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

- **1942.01** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hide-the-last-sack` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Hedwig把最后一袋黑麦埋进煤堆下面，夜里听见巡逻队从窗前走过。第二天面包房照常开门，但货架上少了三排面包；Hedwig知道那不是因为城里没有粮。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +hidden_grain; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 25 -> 35; reputation -0.040000000000000036; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1942.01** `kernel-family.courtship::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/courtship.promised-elsewhere` tags: task, task:family, family, courtship, matchmaking, social, vienna, urban
  Otto Winter 2回话说，对方家里已经应了另一门亲，只是还没下聘。Hedwig把准备好的小礼物——一块香皂和一双线手套——留在柜台上，没有托人送去；第二天上工时，Emil Weiss 3从对面走过，两人都装作在看橱窗。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; cash -1 (soap and thread gloves left at the shop counter); role; flag +courtship_refused; knowledge “对方家已经应了另一门亲，只差下聘。”_

- **1942.04** `r3-crime.vigilante-justice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/untied-the-rope` tags: crime, vigilante, violence, village, law, vienna, urban, crisis
  Luise Bauer把绳子往木桩上绕第三圈时，Hedwig走过去解开了结。Emil Bauer 3的脸已经肿了，嘴里塞着一只袜子。Friedrich Winter赶来时，人群还没有散。Luise Bauer说“你护着Emil Bauer 3，明天丢的就是你家”，Hedwig把绳子扔在地上，说“那就丢”。
  _effects: condition; flag +opposed_vigilante_justice; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000553:child; knowledge “the suspect was tied up behind the mill without evidence”_

- **1942.07** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.buy` tags: home, market, food, social
  六个十字币换来一条鳟鱼和一把欧芹。Hedwig把鱼煎得两面焦黄，鱼尾巴给了闻着味来的Emil Weiss 3。自家孩子用面包把盘底的黄油擦干净。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鳟鱼和黄油); food; mood 0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

- **1942.10** `r3-mig.political-exile::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/met-compatriot-in-exile` tags: migration, exile, politics, safe_house, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena认出了Hedwig，在楼梯间低声问家里怎么样。Hedwig说不知道，出来以后没有收到信。Lena把半包烟塞过来，说“别在餐厅吃饭，那里有人记名字”。Hedwig把烟收下，没有告诉他自己的真名。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +met_compatriot_in_exile; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the hotel restaurant is watched”_

- **1943.01** `r15-curse.aftermath::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/fear-spreads` tags: curse, fear, supernatural, revenge, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Ferdinand Lechner病倒以后，楼里开始有人避开Hedwig。没有人说是诅咒，但每个人都在数Hedwig最近和谁说过话。
  _effects: reputation -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 35 -> 45; reputation -0.040000000000000036; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱)_

- **1943.01** `r13-civ.collect-signatures::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/rejected-for-addresses` tags: politics, petition, signatures, institution, neighborhood, vienna, urban, crisis
  Helene Weiss把签名纸翻过来，说地址不全，有几页只写了楼号没有门牌。Hedwig说那些人怕报复，Helene Weiss说怕报复就别签名。纸被退回来，边角已经卷了。
  _effects: flag +petition_rejected_for_missing_addresses; knowledge “the office rejects petitions without a full address for ever”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

- **1943.08** `r1-civ.ration-scale::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/short-weight-bread` tags: scarcity, rationing, shop, cheating, bread, vienna, urban, crisis
  Katharina Winter把面包放上秤，秤盘底下粘着一块铁，指针刚好停在规定分量。Hedwig看见了，没有说破，只把自己那份掰开，发现里面还是生的。Katharina Berg在队伍后面喊“快点”，Katharina Winter把铁块用指头挪了挪。
  _effects: food; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +short_weight_bread; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001119:child; knowledge “the scale pan hides a piece of iron under the rim”_

- **1943.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil Winter 2嫌Hedwig的床单滴水滴到Emil Winter 2家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Hedwig下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Hedwig把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Emil Winter 2那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

- **1944.01** `curse15-aftermath.the-inquiry::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/a-statement-is-taken` tags: witchcraft, investigation, reputation, rumor, legal, aftermath, vienna, urban
  Helene Weiss把Hedwig叫进办公室，桌上摊着Anton Winter 2交来的三页证词。Hedwig逐条回答，Helene Weiss逐条记录，写到“是否曾诅咒他人”时停了很久。最后Hedwig被允许回家，但门口多了一个每月来一次的访客。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 45 -> 70; state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 30 -> 40; condition; reputation -0.050000000000000044; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1944.01** `r2-faith.spirit-photograph::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/saw-the-second-face` tags: faith, spiritualism, photography, grief, fraud, vienna, urban, crisis
  Katharina Winter 2在暗房里晃了晃底片，说显影时多出来一张脸，站在Hedwig母亲身后。照片洗出来，那张脸没有下巴，像被水泡过。Hedwig把照片放在枕下，夜里摸到相纸的边角，觉得比白天暖。Hermann Weiss看了半天，说像，又说不像。
  _effects: cash -3 (底片上的第二张脸); mood 0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +bought_spirit_photograph; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117; knowledge “the extra face appeared during development, not in the studi”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/whispers-fade` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  一年过去，Hedwig没有再点蜡烛，也没有再去那家后间。教堂的登记册翻到了新的一页，旧页边缘留着指甲划过的痕迹；街上的人开始讨论别的事。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 40 -> 32; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.introduce-two-people/overpraise` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  介绍时把两边都夸过了头，说老友是印厂里最靠得住的人，说女工的手艺整条街都找不出第二个。话太多，两人反倒拘谨起来，各自看窗外。咖啡凉了，坐了不到一个钟头就散了，出门时谁也没提再见面。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和点心); relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1944.06** `r15-ordinary.quiet-year::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/small-good-day` tags: ordinary, daily, family, leisure, peace, vienna, urban, crisis
  这一年没有发生什么大事。Hedwig把窗台擦干净，邻居送来一把樱桃，孩子在走廊里跑来跑去。晚上熄灯前，Hedwig想起一件很久以前的事，笑了一下。
  _effects: food; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +ordinary_good_year_

- **1945.01** `r15-curse.aftermath::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/fear-spreads` tags: curse, fear, supernatural, revenge, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Josef Berg病倒以后，楼里开始有人避开Hedwig。没有人说是诅咒，但每个人都在数Hedwig最近和谁说过话。

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Hedwig躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Hedwig去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

- **1945.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Hedwig胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1945.01** `state_expire:suspected-witchcraft` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  Hedwig的「被疑巫术」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:170_

- **1945.01** `r13-fam.arrange-childcare::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/childcare-arranged` tags: task, care, childcare, work, neighbor, wages, vienna, urban
  Hedwig天没亮就把Ferdinand Kral裹好，抱到楼下Elisabeth Stein家。Elisabeth Stein接过孩子，先摸了摸尿布，又指给Hedwig看灶上温着的米汤。Hedwig把一星期的工钱数出一份放在柜上，Elisabeth Stein数也没数就收进围裙；出门时，Hedwig听见孩子在屋里哭了一声，又停了。
  _effects: cash -2 (上工前把小的送下楼); condition; flag +arranged_daily_childcare; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; knowledge “the minder keeps a pot of rice water on the stove”_

- **1945.05** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-deposit` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  窗子推开只有一线天光，墙纸边角翘起，Hedwig用指甲一刮便掉下一层湿粉。看门人揣好定金，说这间是朝北的，冬天火炉要添得勤些。Josef Weiss 2站在楼梯口没进屋，只替Hedwig看着箱子。
  _effects: cash -4 (当场付下定金和钥匙钱); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1945.11** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Josef Berg把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Hedwig把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Josef Berg低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001979:child; flag +rent_increase_accepted; health -0.02 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase)_

- **1946.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009463_

- **1946.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/dismissed-as-nerves` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig把夜里咳醒的次数写在一张包装纸上，Rosa Stein 2扫了一眼，说是心事太重，给了几包安神粉。走廊尽头的护士压低声音，说住院部还有空床，但要医生签字。Hedwig把包装纸折回去，没再问。
  _effects: cash -1 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); mood -0.039999999999999925; flag +clinic_dismissed_as_nerves; knowledge “the clinic doctor refused a hospital referral”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-135_

- **1946.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Franz Lechner把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Hedwig去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05000000000000001 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-franz-lechner_

- **1946.05** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.send-out` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  洗衣妇把工服从报纸里抖出来，翻开领口看了看油垢，点头收下。Hedwig转身回屋，铜板在围裙口袋里磕出轻响。Rosa Winter还在楼梯上嘟囔，说这钱不如买半斤猪油。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002269:child_

- **1946.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Hedwig下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1946.09** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Hedwig在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Hedwig去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为接手后的俱乐部的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

  Katharina Weiss在通行证上盖了章，又用铅笔在角落写了一个日期。Hedwig拿到许可，可以去城西的修配厂上工；走出劳动局时，Hedwig把证件摸了三遍，确认它还在口袋里。
  _effects: cash -2 (通行证手续费); role; flag +work_permit_granted; knowledge “城西修配厂只认盖蓝章的通行证。”_

- **1947.01** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  窗外有人来问入会，接待的人一时不知道怎么开口，Hedwig替他说“进来坐”。老成员把账本拿回去，用一块旧布包好，放进抽屉最里面。抽屉关上的声音比平时响。
  _effects: flag +union_debt_written_off_

- **1947.01** `r1-hc.home-fever::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fever-breaks` tags: health, fever, home, care, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  夜里Hedwig烧到四十度，Anton Winter 3把醋倒在毛巾上，一遍遍擦胳膊窝。窗玻璃结着霜，炉上的水壶却叫个不停。第三天早晨Hedwig自己坐起来要水喝，嘴唇裂了口，第一句话是问今天谁去上工。
  _effects: cash -3 (水银停在四十度); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; knowledge “vinegar rubs and steam were the whole treatment at home”_

- **1947.01** `r13-civ.ration-card::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ration-card-issued` tags: politics, rationing, food, war, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Katharina Weiss把配给证从本子上撕下来，填上Hedwig家的门牌和人数，又用打孔机在角上打了一个洞。Hedwig把证举到灯下，看见上面印着面粉、糖和煤的数量，每一项都比去年少。
  _effects: food; flag +ration_card_issued; knowledge “the monthly ration is smaller than last year’s”_

- **1947.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Josef Weiss说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Josef Weiss陪着Hedwig去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

  Hedwig把最后一袋黑麦埋进煤堆下面，夜里听见巡逻队从窗前走过。第二天面包房照常开门，但货架上少了三排面包；Hedwig知道那不是因为城里没有粮。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `kernel-civic.petition-drive::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/civic-petition-filed` tags: politics, petition, reform, neighbor, organization, vienna, urban, postwar
  她在一家面包店门口摆开签名纸，Alois Bauer 2第一个签，写完又把丈夫的名字也添上。到傍晚，纸上有一百二十个名字；Katharina Weiss收下时数了两遍，说“我们会研究”。回家的电车上，她发现签名纸的边角被汗浸软了。
  _effects: role; reputation 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; cash -1 (签名纸、墨水和面包店门口的租金); knowledge “请愿书要按街道分开，窗口职员会当面数签名”; flag +petition_filed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

- **1948.02** `p9-history-union-political.meeting/meeting-speak-up` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  Hedwig站起来报出自己车间的计件工价：糊一百个纸盒二十八赫勒，比隔壁车间少六个。屋里一下子静了，Friedrich Winter把草稿推过来，让书记员把这几个数字写在动议第一行。散会时有人拍了拍Hedwig的肩膀。
  _effects: reputation 0.040000000000000036; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000134:child_

- **1948.02** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Franz Lechner手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Franz Lechner把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Hedwig记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner_

- **1948.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Maria Lechner抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Hedwig把床单对折，腾出一半，Maria Lechner用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001492:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Katharina Weiss来问过一次话。问题围绕Berta Berg的病，也围绕Hedwig最近有没有说过什么威胁的话。Hedwig如实回答，Katharina Weiss把每个名字都写进了本子。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:235_

- **1949.01** `r13-fam.invite-cafe::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/coffee-accepted` tags: task, social, cafe, friendship, invitation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig在柜台前把两枚硬币捏出汗，才开口请Grete Huber喝一杯。Grete Huber挑了靠窗的小桌，把糖块推给Hedwig，说这里比家里暖和。两人坐到灯亮，杯底的渣子凉透了，Hedwig还是没有把那句“以后常来”说出口。
  _effects: cash -2 (靠窗那张小桌); mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +bought_a_friend_coffee; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001448:child_

- **1949.03** `r2-rural.well-contamination::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::reform-new-pump/signed-for-the-pump` tags: rural, water, health, neighbor, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien的人带来一台手压泵，说公署出三分之二，剩下的按户摊。Hedwig把分摊的钱数念给Mitzi听，Mitzi说这比淘井便宜。装泵那天，孩子们围着铁管，等第一股水出来。
  _effects: cash -5 (井里的死老鼠); flag +installed_village_pump; relation colleague with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; organization_membership_

- **1949.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.stitched` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Hedwig用鞋蜡把裂缝填上，又拿粗针和麻线把鞋底缝了一圈。针脚歪，鞋底一高一低，走路时右脚落地总比左脚重。第二天上工回来，脚后跟磨出一个水泡，可鞋没再渗水。
  _effects: skill_

- **1950.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sold-the-letter` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, vienna, urban
  Friedrich Lechner把Hedwig托付保管的一封旧信卖给了收旧纸的人。信里提到几个已经不用的名字，买主又把信转给了另一个买主。Hedwig再见到那封信时，它被压在一块玻璃下面，标价是Hedwig半个月的工钱。
  _effects: cash -1 (买回自己的旧信); reputation -0.050000000000000044; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003378:child; knowledge “我写的一封旧信被人压在玻璃下面标价出售。”_

- **1950.01** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig听见墙里有一声很轻的响。第二天，Friedrich Lechner病倒了；没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 1 -> 2; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011999_

- **1950.01** `state_expire:under-investigation` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick, state:under-investigation
  Hedwig的「被调查」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:235_

- **1950.01** `r13-work.file-invoice::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/invoice-lost` tags: commerce, invoice, bureaucracy, payment, literacy, vienna, urban, postwar
  发票交上去两个月，钱一直没有到。Hedwig去问，Franz Bauer说单据在传递时丢了，让Hedwig补一份。补写的发票没有原始收货单，Franz Bauer只肯先付一半。Hedwig回作坊翻遍抽屉，找到一张被油墨弄脏的送货单，边角上还有Franz Bauer的签名。
  _effects: cash -2 (复写纸下的发票); flag +work13_invoice_lost; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; knowledge “a signed delivery note survives in the workshop drawer”_

- **1950.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Hedwig在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129_

- **1950.10** `statistical_infection`
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1950.11** `r2-faith.confession-seal::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/kept-the-seal` tags: faith, confession, secrecy, police, dread, vienna, urban, postwar
  仓库起火那晚，Hedwig在告解帘外整理蜡烛，听见里面的人说自己划了火柴。第二天Katharina Weiss来问话，Hedwig说只听见风声。Anton Winter 3把圣器柜的钥匙放在Hedwig手心，说有些话进了帘子，就该烂在帘子里。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; condition; flag +kept_confession_seal_under_police_pressure; knowledge “the confessed arsonist described the warehouse on Hafner Lan”_

- **1950.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Hedwig的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1951.01** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/name-the-neighbor` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Hedwig告诉Grete Bauer，Friedrich 2家的地窖里还有两桶猪油。征用队下午就去了那户人家。Hedwig拿到一小袋盐和一张优先配给卡，回家时没有走平时那条街。
  _effects: cash 1 (检举邻居得到的配给奖励); reputation 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002755:child; flag +denounced_neighbor_for_grain_

- **1951.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.got-ticket` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Hedwig挤到窗口前，把一克朗硬币推进凹槽，换回一张印着座号的薄纸片。顶层站席的栏杆被前面的人汗湿，Hedwig侧身找了个能看见舞台边的位置，倚着烫手的铁栏看完两幕；散场时小腿发麻，嘴里还哼着刚听到的曲调，幕间也和邻座搭上了两句话。
  _effects: cash -1 (约瑟夫施塔特剧院顶层站票); mood 0.05999999999999994; skill_

- **1951.02** `r2-law.eviction-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::r2-law.eviction-hearing-reform/habitability_inspection` tags: law, eviction, housing, court, tenement, vienna, urban, postwar
  Katharina Weiss派了一名检查员去看房，回来说厕所漏水、电线裸露，不适合居住。Anna Weiss 2说可以降租，Katharina Weiss说降租也要先修。Hedwig问修房期间住哪里，Katharina Weiss说可以申请临时安置；安置名单排到明年，Mitzi Berg说先住我家阁楼。
  _effects: cash -4 (驱逐听证的十五分钟); condition; flag +eviction_case_referred_to_housing_inspection; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004268:child; knowledge “the temporary housing list runs into next year”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

- **1951.02** `statistical_recovery`
  Hedwig从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1951.04** `r1-fam.inheritance-dispute::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/inheritance.split` tags: family, inheritance, will, court, workshop, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig不想打官司，把工具分成两堆，让Anton先挑。Anton挑走了刨子和凿子，留下那台旧车床；Hedwig把车床擦了一遍，发现底座下面压着父亲刻的一行小字：给肯留下来的那个。Katharina Weiss在旁边看着，没有说话。
  _effects: cash 2 (封蜡下面的名字); flag +split_the_fathers_tools; role; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “父亲在车床底下刻了一行字：给肯留下来的那个。”_

- **1951.05** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig和Anna Bauer一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Hedwig去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Hedwig先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1952.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/counts-the-interval` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  Hedwig把两次背叛和两次发烧的日期写在同一张纸上，发现中间都隔着十一天。Hedwig没有告诉任何人，只把纸折成很小的一块，塞进缝衣盒的夹层。那天夜里，Hedwig第一次试着在门口撒盐，动作熟练得让自己停了一下。
  _effects: knowledge “背叛与发烧之间隔着十一天。”; skill; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig把通知念给Maria Lechner听，Maria Lechner摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Hedwig去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.06** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Anna Bauer追出来问怎么不领了，Hedwig说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Hedwig把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; food_

- **1952.07** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.buy-coal` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  炉门一开，黑烟先扑出来，Hedwig眯着眼把纸团往里塞。火苗舔着碎煤慢慢亮起来，Otto Winter 3隔着门喊当心一氧化碳。水壶开始咕噜作响，屋里才有点热乎气。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_
